[No.56] Uncertainty Estimation for Safety-critical Scene Segmentation via Fine-grained Reward Maximization
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过细粒度奖励最大化进行安全关键场景分割的不确定性估计
类别: 
作者: Hongzheng Yang;Cheng Chen;Yueyao Chen;Markus Scheppach;Hon Chi Yip;Qi Dou;
机构: Harvard University;Chinese University of Hong Kong;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70225
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.02719
英摘: Uncertainty estimation plays an important role for future reliable deployment of deep segmentation models in safety-critical scenarios such as medical applications. However, existing methods for uncertainty estimation have been limited by the lack of explicit guidance for calibrating the prediction risk and model confidence. In this work, we propose a novel fine-grained reward maximization (FGRM) framework, to address uncertainty estimation by directly utilizing an uncertainty metric related reward function with a reinforcement learning based model tuning algorithm. This would benefit the model uncertainty estimation through direct optimization guidance for model calibration. Specifically, our method designs a new uncertainty estimation reward function using the calibration metric, which is maximized to fine-tune an evidential learning pre-trained segmentation model for calibrating prediction risk. Importantly, we innovate an effective fine-grained parameter update scheme, which imposes fine-grained reward-weighting of each network parameter according to the parameter importance quantified by the fisher information matrix. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work exploring reward optimization for model uncertainty estimation in safety-critical vision tasks. The effectiveness of our method is demonstrated on two large safety-critical surgical scene segmentation datasets under two different uncertainty estimation settings. With real-time one forward pass at inference, our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods by a clear margin on all the calibration metrics of uncertainty estimation, while maintaining a high task accuracy for the segmentation results. Code is available at \url{https://github.com/med-air/FGRM}.
中摘: 

不确定性估计对于深度分割模型在未来安全关键场景（如医疗应用）中的可靠部署至关重要。然而，现有的不确定性估计方法受限于缺乏校准预测风险和模型置信度的明确指导。在此工作中，我们提出了一种新颖的细粒度奖励最大化（FGRM）框架，旨在通过直接利用与不确定性指标相关的奖励函数，结合基于强化学习的模型调整算法来解决不确定性估计问题。这将通过对模型校准的直接优化指导来促进模型不确定性估计。具体而言，我们的方法利用校准指标设计了一种新的不确定性估计奖励函数，通过最大化该函数来微调基于证据学习的预训练分割模型，以校准预测风险。重要的是，我们创新了一种有效的细粒度参数更新方案，根据由费雪信息矩阵量化的参数重要性，对每个网络参数施加细粒度的奖励加权。据我们所知，这是首个探索安全关键视觉任务中模型不确定性估计奖励优化的工作。我们的方法的有效性在两个大型安全关键手术场景分割数据集上，在两种不同的不确定性估计设置下得到了验证。在推理时仅需单次前向传播，我们的方法在所有不确定性估计的校准指标上均以显著优势优于最先进的方法，同时保持了分割结果的高任务准确率。代码可在 https://github.com/med-air/FGRM 获取。

[No.58] Act As You Wish: Fine-Grained Control of Motion Diffusion Model with Hierarchical Semantic Graphs
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 按您的意愿行动：基于层次语义图的精细控制运动扩散模型
类别: 
作者: Peng Jin;Yang Wu;Yanbo Fan;Zhongqian Sun;Yang Wei;Li Yuan;
机构: Peking University;Tencent;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/70005
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.01015
英摘: Most text-driven human motion generation methods employ sequential modeling approaches, e.g., transformer, to extract sentence-level text representations automatically and implicitly for human motion synthesis. However, these compact text representations may overemphasize the action names at the expense of other important properties and lack fine-grained details to guide the synthesis of subtly distinct motion. In this paper, we propose hierarchical semantic graphs for fine-grained control over motion generation. Specifically, we disentangle motion descriptions into hierarchical semantic graphs including three levels of motions, actions, and specifics. Such global-to-local structures facilitate a comprehensive understanding of motion description and fine-grained control of motion generation. Correspondingly, to leverage the coarse-to-fine topology of hierarchical semantic graphs, we decompose the text-to-motion diffusion process into three semantic levels, which correspond to capturing the overall motion, local actions, and action specifics. Extensive experiments on two benchmark human motion datasets, including HumanML3D and KIT, with superior performances, justify the efficacy of our method. More encouragingly, by modifying the edge weights of hierarchical semantic graphs, our method can continuously refine the generated motion, which may have a far-reaching impact on the community. Code and pre-training weights are available at https://github.com/jpthu17/GraphMotion.
中摘: 

大多数文本驱动的人体运动生成方法采用序列建模方法，例如 Transformer，以自动且隐式地提取句子级文本表示用于人体运动合成。然而，这些紧凑的文本表示可能会过度强调动作名称，而牺牲其他重要属性，并且缺乏细粒度细节来指导微妙差异运动的合成。在本文中，我们提出了分层语义图，以实现对运动生成的细粒度控制。具体而言，我们将运动描述解耦为包含运动、动作和细节三个层级的分层语义图。这种从全局到局部的结构有助于全面理解运动描述并实现对运动生成的细粒度控制。相应地，为了利用分层语义图从粗到细的拓扑结构，我们将文到运动扩散过程分解为三个语义层级，分别对应捕捉整体运动、局部动作和动作细节。在包括 HumanML3D 和 KIT 在内的两个基准人体运动数据集上进行的广泛实验，凭借其优越的性能，证明了我们方法的有效性。更令人鼓舞的是，通过修改分层语义图的边权重，我们的方法可以持续优化生成的运动，这可能对该社区产生深远影响。代码和预训练权重可通过 https://github.com/jpthu17/GraphMotion 获取。

[No.59] Learning Fine-grained View-Invariant Representations from Unpaired Ego-Exo Videos via Temporal Alignment
来源: NeurIPS 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过时间对齐学习细粒度的视角不变表示来自未配对的第一人称与第三人称视频
类别: 
作者: Zihui Xue;Kristen Grauman;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;
类型: Poster
链接: https://nips.cc/virtual/2023/poster/71749
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2306.05526
英摘: The egocentric and exocentric viewpoints of a human activity look dramatically different, yet invariant representations to link them are essential for many potential applications in robotics and augmented reality. Prior work is limited to learning view-invariant features from paired synchronized viewpoints. We relax that strong data assumption and propose to learn fine-grained action features that are invariant to the viewpoints by aligning egocentric and exocentric videos in time, even when not captured simultaneously or in the same environment. To this end, we propose AE2, a self-supervised embedding approach with two key designs: (1) an object-centric encoder that explicitly focuses on regions corresponding to hands and active objects; and (2) a contrastive-based alignment objective that leverages temporally reversed frames as negative samples. For evaluation, we establish a benchmark for fine-grained video understanding in the ego-exo context, comprising four datasets -- including an ego tennis forehand dataset we collected, along with dense per-frame labels we annotated for each dataset. On the four datasets, our AE2 method strongly outperforms prior work in a variety of fine-grained downstream tasks, both in regular and cross-view settings.
中摘: 

人类活动的第一人称视角与外部视角看似截然不同，但能够将两者联系起来的不变表征对于机器人与增强现实领域的许多潜在应用至关重要。既往工作仅限于从配对的同步视角中学习视角不变特征。我们放宽了这一强数据假设，提出通过对第一人称和外部视角视频进行时序对齐，学习对视角不变的细粒度动作特征，即使这些视频并非同时采集或处于相同环境中。为此，我们提出了 AE2，一种自监督嵌入方法，包含两个关键设计：(1) 一个以物体为中心的编码器，明确关注对应于手部和活动物体的区域；以及 (2) 一个基于对比的对齐目标，利用时间反转帧作为负样本。为了评估，我们建立了第一人称 - 外部视角上下文下的细粒度视频理解基准，包含四个数据集——其中包括我们收集的一个第一人称网球正手数据集，以及我们为每个数据集标注的稠密逐帧标签。在这四个数据集上，我们的 AE2 方法在多种细粒度下游任务中均显著优于既往工作，涵盖常规设置与跨视角设置。

[No.84] Davidsonian Scene Graph: Improving Reliability in Fine-grained Evaluation for Text-to-Image Generation
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 大卫森场景图：提高文本到图像生成精细评估的可靠性
类别: representation learning for computer vision, audio, language, and other modalities
作者: Jaemin Cho;Yushi Hu;Jason Michael Baldridge;Roopal Garg;Peter Anderson;Ranjay Krishna;Mohit Bansal;Jordi Pont-Tuset;Su Wang;
机构: University of North Carolina;University of Washington;Google;Balyasny Asset Management;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18963
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.18235
英摘: Evaluating text-to-image models is notoriously difficult. A strong recent approach for assessing text-image faithfulness is based on QG/A (question generation and answering), which uses pre-trained foundational models to automatically generate a set of questions and answers from the prompt, and output images are scored based on whether these answers extracted with a visual question answering model are consistent with the prompt-based answers. This kind of evaluation is naturally dependent on the quality of the underlying QG and VQA models. We identify and address several reliability challenges in existing QG/A work: (a) QG questions should respect the prompt (avoiding hallucinations, duplications, and omissions) and (b) VQA answers should be consistent (not asserting that there is no motorcycle in an image while also claiming the motorcycle is blue). We address these issues with Davidsonian Scene Graph (DSG), an empirically grounded evaluation framework inspired by formal semantics, which is adaptable to any QG/A frameworks. DSG produces atomic and unique questions organized in dependency graphs, which (i) ensure appropriate semantic coverage and (ii) sidestep inconsistent answers. With extensive experimentation and human evaluation on a range of model configurations (LLM, VQA, and T2I), we empirically demonstrate that DSG addresses the challenges noted above. Finally, we present DSG-1k, an open-sourced evaluation benchmark that includes 1,060 prompts, covering a wide range of fine-grained semantic categories with a balanced distribution. We release the DSG-1k prompts and the corresponding DSG questions.
中摘: 

评估文本生成图像模型极其困难。近期一种强有力的评估文本 - 图像忠实度的方法基于问答生成与回答（Question Generation and Answering, QG/A），该方法利用预训练基础模型根据提示词自动生成一组问题和答案，并根据通过视觉问答模型提取的答案是否与基于提示词生成的答案一致，对输出图像进行评分。此类评估自然依赖于底层问答生成和视觉问答模型的质量。我们识别并解决了现有 QG/A 工作中的几个可靠性挑战：(a) QG 问题应遵循提示词（避免幻觉、重复和遗漏），(b) VQA 答案应保持一致（例如，不应既声称图像中不存在摩托车，又声称摩托车是蓝色的）。我们使用大卫森场景图（Davidsonian Scene Graph, DSG）解决了这些问题。DSG 是一个受形式语义学启发、具有实证基础的评估框架，适用于任何 QG/A 框架。DSG 生成以依赖图组织的原子且唯一的问题，这既 (i) 确保了适当的语义覆盖，又 (ii) 规避了不一致的答案。通过对一系列模型配置（大语言模型 LLM、视觉问答 VQA 和文本生成图像 T2I）进行广泛的实验和人工评估，我们实证证明了 DSG 解决了上述挑战。最后，我们推出了 DSG-1k，这是一个包含 1,060 个提示词的开源评估基准，涵盖了广泛的细粒度语义类别，且分布均衡。我们发布了 DSG-1k 提示词及其相应的 DSG 问题。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.85] Respect the model: Fine-grained and Robust Explanation with Sharing Ratio Decomposition
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 尊重模型：细粒度和稳健的解释方法与共享比率分解
类别: visualization or interpretation of learned representations
作者: Sangyu Han;Yearim Kim;Nojun Kwak;
机构: Seoul National University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/18533
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.03348
英摘: The truthfulness of existing explanation methods in authentically elucidating the underlying model's decision-making process has been questioned. Existing methods have deviated from faithfully representing the model, thus susceptible to adversarial attacks. To address this, we propose a novel eXplainable AI (XAI) method called SRD (Sharing Ratio Decomposition), which sincerely reflects the model's inference process, resulting in significantly enhanced robustness in our explanations. Different from the conventional emphasis on the neuronal level, we adopt a vector perspective to consider the intricate nonlinear interactions between filters. We also introduce an interesting observation termed Activation-Pattern-Only Prediction (APOP), letting us emphasize the importance of inactive neurons and redefine relevance encapsulating all relevant information including both active and inactive neurons. Our method, SRD, allows for the recursive decomposition of a Pointwise Feature Vector (PFV), providing a high-resolution Effective Receptive Field (ERF) at any layer.
中摘: 

现有解释方法在真实阐明潜在模型决策过程方面的真实性已受到质疑。现有方法未能忠实地表征模型，因而易受对抗攻击。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为 SRD（共享比率分解）的新型可解释人工智能（XAI）方法，该方法忠实地反映了模型的推理过程，从而显著增强了解释的鲁棒性。不同于传统侧重于神经元层级，我们采用向量视角来考量滤波器之间错综复杂的非线性交互。此外，我们还引入了一项有趣的观察，称为“仅激活模式预测”（APOP），使我们能够强调不活跃神经元的重要性，并重新定义了相关性，该定义囊括了所有相关信息，包括活跃和不活跃神经元。我们的方法 SRD 允许对逐点特征向量（PFV）进行递归分解，从而在任意层级提供高分辨率的有效感受野（ERF）。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.86] Protein-ligand binding representation learning from fine-grained interactions
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 从细粒度相互作用学习蛋白质-配体结合表示
类别: unsupervised, self-supervised, semi-supervised, and supervised representation learning
作者: Shikun Feng;Minghao Li;Yinjun Jia;Wei-Ying Ma;Yanyan Lan;
机构: Tsinghua University;University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;
类型: Poster
链接: https://iclr.cc/virtual/2024/poster/19258
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.16160
英摘: The binding between proteins and ligands plays a crucial role in the realm of drug discovery. Previous deep learning approaches have shown promising results over traditional computationally intensive methods, but resulting in poor generalization due to limited supervised data. In this paper, we propose to learn protein-ligand binding representation in a self-supervised learning manner. Different from existing pre-training approaches which treat proteins and ligands individually, we emphasize to discern the intricate binding patterns from fine-grained interactions. Specifically, this self-supervised learning problem is formulated as a prediction of the conclusive binding complex structure given a pocket and ligand with a Transformer based interaction module, which naturally emulates the binding process. To ensure the representation of rich binding information, we introduce two pre-training tasks, i.e.~atomic pairwise distance map prediction and mask ligand reconstruction, which comprehensively model the fine-grained interactions from both structure and feature space. Extensive experiments have demonstrated the superiority of our method across various binding tasks, including protein-ligand affinity prediction, virtual screening and protein-ligand docking.
中摘: 

蛋白质与配体的结合在药物发现领域发挥着至关重要的作用。先前的深度学习方法相较于传统的计算密集型方法已取得显著成果，但受限于监督数据不足，往往导致泛化能力较差。本文提出采用自监督学习的方式学习蛋白质 - 配体的结合表示。与现有单独处理蛋白质和配体的预训练方法不同，我们强调从细粒度相互作用中识别复杂的结合模式。具体而言，该自监督学习问题被构建为：利用基于 Transformer 的交互模块，给定结合口袋和配体以预测最终的结合复合物结构，这自然地模拟了结合过程。为确保能够表征丰富的结合信息，我们引入了两个预训练任务，即原子成对距离图预测和掩蔽配体重建，这两个任务从结构和特征空间全面地建模了细粒度相互作用。大量实验证明，我们的方法在包括蛋白质 - 配体亲和力预测、虚拟筛选和蛋白质 - 配体对接在内的各种结合任务中均表现出优越性。

[No.87] Prometheus: Inducing Fine-Grained Evaluation Capability in Language Models
来源: ICLR 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 普罗米修斯：在语言模型中诱导细粒度评估能力
类别: datasets and benchmarks
作者: Seungone Kim;Jamin Shin;Yejin Cho;Joel Jang;Shayne Longpre;Hwaran Lee;Sangdoo Yun;Seongjin Shin;Sungdong Kim;James Thorne;Minjoon Seo;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;NAVER Corporation;University of Washington;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;Twelve Labs;
类型: Poster

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.08491
英摘: Recently, using a powerful proprietary Large Language Model (LLM) (e.g., GPT-4) as an evaluator for long-form responses has become the de facto standard. However, for practitioners with large-scale evaluation tasks and custom criteria in consideration (e.g., child-readability), using proprietary LLMs as an evaluator is unreliable due to the closed-source nature, uncontrolled versioning, and prohibitive costs. In this work, we propose Prometheus, a fully open-source LLM that is on par with GPT-4's evaluation capabilities when the appropriate reference materials (reference answer, score rubric) are accompanied. We first construct the Feedback Collection, a new dataset that consists of 1K fine-grained score rubrics, 20K instructions, and 100K responses and language feedback generated by GPT-4. Using the Feedback Collection, we train Prometheus, a 13B evaluator LLM that can assess any given long-form text based on customized score rubric provided by the user. Experimental results show that Prometheus scores a Pearson correlation of 0.897 with human evaluators when evaluating with 45 customized score rubrics, which is on par with GPT-4 (0.882), and greatly outperforms ChatGPT (0.392). Furthermore, measuring correlation with GPT-4 with 1222 customized score rubrics across four benchmarks (MT Bench, Vicuna Bench, Feedback Bench, Flask Eval) shows similar trends, bolstering Prometheus's capability as an evaluator LLM. Lastly, Prometheus achieves the highest accuracy on two human preference benchmarks (HHH Alignment & MT Bench Human Judgment) compared to open-sourced reward models explicitly trained on human preference datasets, highlighting its potential as an universal reward model. We open-source our code, dataset, and model at https://kaistai.github.io/prometheus/.
中摘: 

最近，使用功能强大的专有大型语言模型（LLM）（如 GPT-4）对长文回复进行评估，已成为事实上的标准。然而，对于拥有大规模评估任务并需考虑自定义标准（例如儿童可读性）的从业者而言，由于专有 LLM 的闭源性质、不可控的版本迭代以及高昂的成本，将其作为评估工具并不可靠。在这项工作中，我们提出了 Prometheus，这是一个完全开源的 LLM，当辅以适当的参考材料（参考答案、评分标准）时，其评估能力可与 GPT-4 相媲美。我们首先构建了 Feedback Collection，这是一个包含 1K 个细粒度评分标准、20K 条指令以及由 GPT-4 生成的 100K 条回复和语言反馈的新数据集。利用 Feedback Collection，我们训练了 Prometheus，这是一个 13B 参数的评估型 LLM，能够根据用户提供的自定义评分标准评估任何给定的长文文本。实验结果表明，在使用 45 个自定义评分标准进行评估时，Prometheus 与人类评估者之间的皮尔逊相关系数达到 0.897，这与 GPT-4（0.882）相当，并显著优于 ChatGPT（0.392）。此外，在四个基准（MT Bench、Vicuna Bench、Feedback Bench、Flask Eval）上使用 1222 个自定义评分标准与 GPT-4 进行相关性测量，结果显示了相似的趋势，进一步巩固了 Prometheus 作为评估型 LLM 的能力。最后，与专门在人类偏好数据集上训练的开源奖励模型相比，Prometheus 在两个基于人类偏好的基准（HHH Alignment 和 MT Bench Human Judgment）上取得了最高的准确率，突显了其作为通用奖励模型的潜力。我们在 https://kaistai.github.io/prometheus/ 开源了我们的代码、数据集和模型。

[No.88] Foundation Model Insights and a Multi-Model Approach for Superior Fine-Grained One-shot Subset Selection
来源: ICML 2025

标题: 基础模型洞察与多模型方法在卓越细粒度一-shot 子集选择中的应用

英摘: One-shot subset selection serves as an effective tool to reduce deep learning training costs by identifying an informative data subset based on the information extracted by an information extractor (IE). Traditional IEs, typically pre-trained on the target dataset, are inherently dataset-dependent. Foundation models (FMs) offer a promising alternative, potentially mitigating this limitation. This work investigates two key questions: (1) Can FM-based subset selection outperform traditional IE-based methods across diverse datasets? (2) Do all FMs perform equally well as IEs for subset selection? Extensive experiments uncovered surprising insights: FMs consistently outperform traditional IEs on fine-grained datasets, whereas their advantage diminishes on coarse-grained datasets with noisy labels. Motivated by these finding, we propose RAM-APL (RAnking Mean-Accuracy of Pseudo-class Labels), a method tailored for fine-grained image datasets. RAM-APL leverages multiple FMs to enhance subset selection by exploiting their complementary strengths. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on fine-grained datasets, including Oxford-IIIT Pet, Food-101, and Caltech-UCSD Birds-200-2011.
中摘: 

单次子集选择是一种有效工具，它通过基于信息提取器（IE）所提取的信息来识别信息丰富的数据子集，从而降低深度学习训练成本。传统的 IE 通常针对目标数据集进行预训练，因此本质上具有数据集依赖性。基础模型（FMs）提供了一种有前景的替代方案，有望缓解这一局限性。本研究探讨了两个关键问题：(1) 基于基础模型的子集选择能否在多样化的数据集上优于传统基于 IE 的方法？(2) 所有基础模型作为 IE 用于子集选择时是否都能表现同样出色？广泛的实验揭示出令人惊喜的见解：在细粒度数据集上，基础模型一致优于传统 IE；然而，在带有噪声标签的粗粒度数据集上，这种优势则有所减弱。受这些发现的启发，我们提出了 RAM-APL（伪类别标签排名平均准确率），这是一种专为细粒度图像数据集量身定制的方法。RAM-APL 利用多个基础模型，通过挖掘它们的互补优势来增强子集选择。我们的方法在细粒度数据集上取得了最先进的性能，包括 Oxford-IIIT Pet、Food-101 和 Caltech-UCSD Birds-200-2011。

This method deploys a tailored set of queries to capture and represent nuanced attribute-level information within the hashing process, thereby enhancing both the interpretability and relevance of each hash bit. [No.92] ProofAug: Efficient Neural Theorem Proving via Fine-grained Proof Structure Analysis
来源: ICML 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: ProofAug：通过精细证明结构分析的高效神经定理证明
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Haoxiong Liu;Jiacheng Sun;Zhenguo Li;Andrew C Yao;

英摘: The synergy between deep learning models and traditional automation tools, such as built-in tactics of the proof assistant and off-the-shelf automated theorem provers, plays a crucial role in developing robust and efficient neural theorem provers(NTPs). However, for proof synthesis with LLMs, previous work applies automation tools either only when explicitly invoked by the model or at a single granularity level, failing to fully exploit their power. To solve this issue, we propose ProofAug, a procedure that equips LLMs with automation methods at various granularities through fine-grained structure analysis of model-generated proof proposals. ProofAug also serves as a versatile plug-and-play module that seamlessly integrates with any tree-search algorithm, enabling our construction of an efficient recursive proving (ERP) module to further enhance performance. The superiority of our method is validated on the miniF2F benchmark using the open-source deepseek-math-7b-base model and the Isabelle proof assistant. We also implement a Lean 4 version of ProofAug that can improve the pass@1 performance of Kimina-Prover-Preview-Distill-1.5B from 44.3% to 50.4% on miniF2F-test. Our code is available at https://github.com/haoxiongliu/ProofAug.

[No.95] BlockDialect: Block-wise Fine-grained Mixed Format Quantization for Energy-Efficient LLM Inference
来源: ICML 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 块级细粒度混合格式量化：面向能效的LLM推理
类别: deep learning->large language models
作者: Wonsuk Jang;Thierry Tambe;
机构: Stanford University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/44936
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01144
英摘: The rapidly increasing size of large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges in memory usage and computational costs. Quantizing both weights and activations can address these issues, with hardware-supported fine-grained scaling emerging as a promising solution to mitigate outliers. However, existing methods struggle to capture nuanced block data distributions. We propose BlockDialect, a block-wise fine-grained mixed format technique that assigns a per-block optimal number format from a formatbook for better data representation. Additionally, we introduce DialectFP4, a formatbook of FP4 variants (akin to dialects) that adapt to diverse data distributions. To leverage this efficiently, we propose a two-stage approach for online DialectFP4 activation quantization. Importantly, DialectFP4 ensures energy efficiency by selecting representable values as scaled integers compatible with low-precision integer arithmetic. BlockDialect achieves 10.78% (7.48%) accuracy gain on the LLaMA3-8B (LLaMA2-7B) model compared to MXFP4 format with lower bit usage per data, while being only 5.45% (2.69%) below full precision even when quantizing full-path matrix multiplication. Focusing on how to represent over how to scale, our work presents a promising path for energy-efficient LLM inference.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLM）规模的快速增长给内存使用和计算成本带来了巨大挑战。对权重和激活进行量化可以解决这些问题，而硬件支持的细粒度缩放正作为一种有前景的方案出现，以缓解异常值的影响。然而，现有方法难以捕捉细微的块级数据分布。我们提出了 BlockDialect，这是一种逐块细粒度混合格式技术，它从格式库中为每个块分配最优的数字格式，以实现更好的数据表示。此外，我们引入了 DialectFP4，这是一个包含 FP4 变体（类似于方言）的格式库，能够适应多样化的数据分布。为了高效利用该技术，我们提出了一种用于在线 DialectFP4 激活量化的两阶段方法。重要的是，DialectFP4 通过选择可表示的值作为与低精度整数运算兼容的缩放整数，确保了能效。BlockDialect 在 LLaMA3-8B (LLaMA2-7B) 模型上相比 MXFP4 格式实现了 10.78% (7.48%) 的精度提升，同时每个数据的位占用更少；即使在量化全路径矩阵乘法时，其精度也仅低于全精度 5.45% (2.69%)。我们的工作侧重于表示而非缩放，为高效的 LLM 推理展示了一条充满前景的道路。

[No.96] On Fine-Grained Distinct Element Estimation
来源: ICML 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 细粒度唯一元素估计
类别: theory->everything else
作者: Ilias Diakonikolas;Daniel Kane;Jasper C.H. Lee;Thanasis Pittas;David Woodruff;Samson Zhou;
机构: University of Wisconsin-Madison;University of California, San Diego;University of California, Davis;University of Wisconsin;Carnegie Mellon University;Texas A&M University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2025/poster/45716
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.22608
英摘: We study the problem of distributed distinct element estimation, where $α$ servers each receive a subset of a universe $[n]$ and aim to compute a $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximation to the number of distinct elements using minimal communication. While prior work establishes a worst-case bound of $Θ\left(α\log n+\fracα{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ bits, these results rely on assumptions that may not hold in practice. We introduce a new parameterization based on the number $C = \fracβ{\varepsilon^2}$ of pairwise collisions, i.e., instances where the same element appears on multiple servers, and design a protocol that uses only $\mathcal{O}\left(α\log n+\frac{\sqrtβ}{\varepsilon^2} \log n\right)$ bits, breaking previous lower bounds when $C$ is small. We further improve our algorithm under assumptions on the number of distinct elements or collisions and provide matching lower bounds in all regimes, establishing $C$ as a tight complexity measure for the problem. Finally, we consider streaming algorithms for distinct element estimation parameterized by the number of items with frequency larger than $1$. Overall, our results offer insight into why statistical problems with known hardness results can be efficiently solved in practice.
中摘: 

我们研究分布式不同元素估计问题，其中 $α$ 个服务器各自接收全域 $[n]$ 的一个子集，并旨在利用最小通信量计算不同元素数量的 $(1+\varepsilon)$-近似。尽管先前工作确立了 $Θ\left(α\log n+\fracα{\varepsilon^2}\right)$ 比特的最坏情况界，但这些结果依赖于在实践中可能不成立的假设。我们引入了一种基于成对碰撞数量 $C = \fracβ{\varepsilon^2}$ 的新参数化方法，即同一元素出现在多个服务器上的实例，并设计了一种仅使用 $\mathcal{O}\left(α\log n+\frac{\sqrtβ}{\varepsilon^2} \log n\right)$ 比特的协议，当 $C$ 较小时突破了先前的下界。我们进一步在关于不同元素数量或碰撞数量的假设下改进了算法，并在所有情形下提供了匹配的下界，确立了 $C$ 作为该问题的紧确复杂度度量。最后，我们考虑了由频率大于 $1$ 的项目数量参数化的不同元素估计流算法。总体而言，我们的结果为为何具有已知困难性结果的统计问题在实践中能够高效解决提供了洞见。

[No.98] Token Cleaning: Fine-Grained Data Selection for LLM Supervised Fine-Tuning
来源: ICML 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 细粒度数据选择：用于LLM监督微调的标记清理

作者: Jinlong Pang;Na Di;Zhaowei Zhu;Jiaheng Wei;Hao Cheng;Chen Qian;Yang Liu;
机构: ;Northeastern University;Docta.ai;Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Hong Kong Baptist University;University of California, Santa Cruz;University of California-Santa Cruz;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.01968
英摘: Recent studies show that in supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of large language models (LLMs), data quality matters more than quantity. While most data cleaning methods concentrate on filtering entire samples, the quality of individual tokens within a sample can vary significantly. After pre-training, even in high-quality samples, patterns or phrases that are not task-related can be redundant, uninformative, or even harmful. Continuing to fine-tune on these patterns may offer limited benefit and even degrade downstream task performance. In this paper, we investigate token quality from a noisy-label perspective and propose a generic token cleaning pipeline for SFT tasks. Our method filters out uninformative tokens while preserving those carrying key task-specific information. Specifically, we first evaluate token quality by examining the influence of model updates on each token, then apply a threshold-based separation. The token influence can be measured in a single pass with a fixed reference model or iteratively with self-evolving reference models. The benefits and limitations of both methods are analyzed theoretically by error upper bounds. Extensive experiments show that our framework consistently improves downstream performance. Code is available at https://github.com/UCSC-REAL/TokenCleaning.
中摘: 

最近的研究表明，在大型语言模型（LLMs）的监督微调（SFT）中，数据质量比数量更为重要。虽然大多数数据清洗方法侧重于过滤整个样本，但样本内部各个词元的质量可能存在显著差异。预训练完成后，即使在高质量样本中，与任务无关的模式或短语也可能冗余、缺乏信息，甚至有害。若继续在这些模式上进行微调，不仅收益有限，甚至可能损害下游任务的性能。在本文中，我们从噪声标签的视角研究词元质量，并提出了一种适用于 SFT 任务的通用词元清洗流程。该方法旨在过滤掉无信息的词元，同时保留承载关键特定任务信息的词元。具体而言，我们首先通过评估模型更新对每个词元的影响来判定词元质量，随后实施基于阈值的筛选。词元影响的测量既可采用固定参考模型进行单次前向传播，也可利用自进化参考模型进行迭代测量。两种方法的优劣与局限性已通过误差上界进行了理论分析。大量实验表明，我们的框架能够一致地提升下游任务性能。代码可在 https://github.com/UCSC-REAL/TokenCleaning 获取。

来源: ICML 2025

标题: 通过场景图 consolidation 实现长视频细粒度字幕生成

英摘: Recent advances in vision-language models have led to impressive progress in caption generation for images and short video clips. However, these models remain constrained by their limited temporal receptive fields, making it difficult to produce coherent and comprehensive captions for long videos. While several methods have been proposed to aggregate information across video segments, they often rely on supervised fine-tuning or incur significant computational overhead. To address these challenges, we introduce a novel framework for long video captioning based on graph consolidation. Our approach first generates segment-level captions, corresponding to individual frames or short video intervals, using off-the-shelf visual captioning models. A lightweight graph-to-text decoder then produces the final video-level caption. This framework effectively extends the temporal understanding capabilities of existing models without requiring any additional fine-tuning on long video datasets. Experimental results show that our method significantly outperforms existing LLM-based consolidation approaches, achieving strong zero-shot performance while substantially reducing computational costs.

[No.100] A Fine-grained Analysis of Fitted Q-evaluation: Beyond Parametric Models
来源: ICML 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 细粒度分析拟合Q评估：超越参数化模型
类别: 
作者: Jiayi Wang;Zhengling Qi;Raymond K. W. Wong;
机构: University of Texas at Dallas;George Washington University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34647
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.10438
英摘: In this paper, we delve into the statistical analysis of the fitted Q-evaluation (FQE) method, which focuses on estimating the value of a target policy using offline data generated by some behavior policy. We provide a comprehensive theoretical understanding of FQE estimators under both parameteric and nonparametric models on the $Q$-function. Specifically, we address three key questions related to FQE that remain largely unexplored in the current literature: (1) Is the optimal convergence rate for estimating the policy value regarding the sample size $n$ ($n^{-1/2}$) achievable for FQE under a non-parametric model with a fixed horizon ($T$)? (2) How does the error bound depend on the horizon $T$? (3) What is the role of the probability ratio function in improving the convergence of FQE estimators? Specifically, we show that under the completeness assumption of $Q$-functions, which is mild in the non-parametric setting, the estimation errors for policy value using both parametric and non-parametric FQE estimators can achieve an optimal rate in terms of $n$. The corresponding error bounds in terms of both $n$ and $T$ are also established. With an additional realizability assumption on ratio functions, the rate of estimation errors can be improved from $T^{1.5}/\sqrt{n}$ to $T/\sqrt{n}$, which matches the sharpest known bound in the current literature under the tabular setting.
中摘: 

在本文中，我们深入探讨了拟合 Q 评估（FQE）方法的统计分析，该方法专注于利用由某种行为策略生成的离线数据来估计目标策略的值。我们在参数化和非参数化 $Q$ 函数模型下，为 FQE 估计量提供了全面的理论理解。具体而言，我们探讨了当前文献中尚未充分探索的三个与 FQE 相关的关键问题：(1) 在非参数化模型且视界固定（$T$）的情况下，FQE 能否实现关于样本量 $n$（$n^{-1/2}$）的策略值估计最优收敛速率？(2) 误差界如何依赖于视界 $T$？(3) 概率比率函数在提升 FQE 估计量收敛性方面扮演何种角色？具体而言，我们表明，在 $Q$ 函数的完备性假设下（该假设在非参数化设定下较为温和），无论是使用参数化还是非参数化 FQE 估计量，策略值的估计误差均可达到关于 $n$ 的最优速率。我们还建立了关于 $n$ 和 $T$ 的相应误差界。此外，若在比率函数上附加可实现性假设，估计误差的速率可从 $T^{1.5}/\sqrt{n}$ 改善至 $T/\sqrt{n}$，这与当前文献中表格设定下已知的最紧界限相吻合。

[No.101] Fine-Grained Causal Dynamics Learning with Quantization for Improving Robustness in Reinforcement Learning
来源: ICML 2024

标题: 细粒度因果动力学习与量化改进强化学习中的鲁棒性

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.03234
英摘: Causal dynamics learning has recently emerged as a promising approach to enhancing robustness in reinforcement learning (RL). Typically, the goal is to build a dynamics model that makes predictions based on the causal relationships among the entities. Despite the fact that causal connections often manifest only under certain contexts, existing approaches overlook such fine-grained relationships and lack a detailed understanding of the dynamics. In this work, we propose a novel dynamics model that infers fine-grained causal structures and employs them for prediction, leading to improved robustness in RL. The key idea is to jointly learn the dynamics model with a discrete latent variable that quantizes the state-action space into subgroups. This leads to recognizing meaningful context that displays sparse dependencies, where causal structures are learned for each subgroup throughout the training. Experimental results demonstrate the robustness of our method to unseen states and locally spurious correlations in downstream tasks where fine-grained causal reasoning is crucial. We further illustrate the effectiveness of our subgroup-based approach with quantization in discovering fine-grained causal relationships compared to prior methods.

[No.106] Improving fine-grained understanding in image-text pre-training
来源: ICML 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 图像-文本预训练中的细粒度理解提升
类别: 
作者: Ioana Bica;Anastasija Ilic;Matthias Bauer;Goker Erdogan;Matko Bošnjak;Christos Kaplanis;Alexey A. Gritsenko;Matthias Minderer;Charles Blundell;Razvan Pascanu;Jovana Mitrovic;
机构: Google;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/34962
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.09865
英摘: We introduce SPARse Fine-grained Contrastive Alignment (SPARC), a simple method for pretraining more fine-grained multimodal representations from image-text pairs. Given that multiple image patches often correspond to single words, we propose to learn a grouping of image patches for every token in the caption. To achieve this, we use a sparse similarity metric between image patches and language tokens and compute for each token a language-grouped vision embedding as the weighted average of patches. The token and language-grouped vision embeddings are then contrasted through a fine-grained sequence-wise loss that only depends on individual samples and does not require other batch samples as negatives. This enables more detailed information to be learned in a computationally inexpensive manner. SPARC combines this fine-grained loss with a contrastive loss between global image and text embeddings to learn representations that simultaneously encode global and local information. We thoroughly evaluate our proposed method and show improved performance over competing approaches both on image-level tasks relying on coarse-grained information, e.g. classification, as well as region-level tasks relying on fine-grained information, e.g. retrieval, object detection, and segmentation. Moreover, SPARC improves model faithfulness and captioning in foundational vision-language models.
中摘: 

我们提出了稀疏细粒度对比对齐（SPARC），这是一种从图像 - 文本对中预训练更细粒度多模态表示的简单方法。鉴于多个图像块通常对应单个单词，我们提出为图像描述中的每个词元学习图像块的分组。为此，我们使用图像块与语言词元之间的稀疏相似度度量，并为每个词元计算语言分组视觉嵌入，即图像块的加权平均。随后，利用细粒度序列损失对词元嵌入与语言分组视觉嵌入进行对比，该损失仅依赖于单个样本，无需其他批次样本作为负样本。这使得能够以计算成本较低的方式学习到更详细的信息。SPARC 将这种细粒度损失与全局图像 - 文本嵌入之间的对比损失相结合，从而学习能够同时编码全局和局部信息的表示。我们全面评估了所提出的方法，结果显示：相较于其他竞争方法，无论是在依赖粗粒度信息的图像级任务（例如分类）上，还是在依赖细粒度信息的区域级任务（例如检索、目标检测和分割）上，性能均有所提升。此外，SPARC 还能提升基础视觉 - 语言模型中的模型忠实度与图像描述生成能力。

英摘: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable proficiency in comprehending and handling text-based tasks. In light of these challenges, we propose Momentor, a Video-LLM capable of accomplishing fine-grained temporal understanding tasks. We train Momentor on Moment-10M, enabling it to perform segment-level reasoning and localization. Zero-shot evaluations on several tasks demonstrate that Momentor excels in fine-grained temporally grounded comprehension and localization.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLMs）在理解和处理基于文本的任务方面展现出卓越的能力。许多工作致力于将这些能力迁移至视频模态，这类模型被称为视频大型语言模型（Video-LLMs）。然而，现有的视频大型语言模型仅能捕捉粗粒度语义，无法有效处理与特定视频片段理解或定位相关的任务。针对这些挑战，我们提出了 Momentor，这是一个能够执行细粒度时间理解任务的视频大型语言模型。为了支持 Momentor 的训练，我们设计了一个自动数据生成引擎，构建了 Moment-10M，这是一个包含片段级指令数据的大规模视频指令数据集。我们在 Moment-10M 上训练 Momentor，使其能够进行片段级推理和定位。在多个任务上的零样本评估表明，Momentor 在细粒度时间锚定理解和定位方面表现卓越。

[No.108] Not all distributional shifts are equal: Fine-grained robust conformal inference
来源: ICML 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 并非所有的分布偏移都相同：细粒度稳健的自适应推断
类别: 
作者: Jiahao Ai;Zhimei Ren;
机构: University of Pennsylvania;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/35127
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.13042
英摘: We introduce a fine-grained framework for uncertainty quantification of predictive models under distributional shifts. This framework distinguishes the shift in covariate distributions from that in the conditional relationship between the outcome ($Y$) and the covariates ($X$). We propose to reweight the training samples to adjust for an identifiable covariate shift while protecting against worst-case conditional distribution shift bounded in an $f$-divergence ball. Based on ideas from conformal inference and distributionally robust learning, we present an algorithm that outputs (approximately) valid and efficient prediction intervals in the presence of distributional shifts. As a use case, we apply the framework to sensitivity analysis of individual treatment effects with hidden confounding. The proposed methods are evaluated in simulation studies and four real data applications, demonstrating superior robustness and efficiency compared with existing benchmarks.
中摘: 

我们提出了一种用于分布偏移下预测模型不确定性量化的细粒度框架。该框架区分了协变量分布的偏移与结果（$Y$）和协变量（$X$）之间条件关系的偏移。我们提议通过对训练样本进行重加权来校正可识别的协变量偏移，同时防范约束于 $f$-散度球内的最坏情况条件分布偏移。基于共形推断和分布鲁棒学习的理念，我们提出了一种算法，能够在存在分布偏移的情况下输出（近似）有效且高效的预测区间。作为一种应用场景，我们将该框架应用于具有隐藏混淆因素的个体处理效应的敏感性分析。所提出的方法在模拟研究和四个真实数据应用案例中进行了评估，结果表明与现有基准相比，该方法具有更优的鲁棒性和效率。

[No.109] On Computational Limits of Modern Hopfield Models: A Fine-Grained Complexity Analysis
来源: ICML 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 关于现代霍普菲尔德模型计算极限的精细复杂性分析
类别: 
作者: Jerry Yao-Chieh Hu;Thomas Lin;Zhao Song;Han Liu;
机构: Adobe;Northwestern University;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32779
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04520
英摘: We investigate the computational limits of the memory retrieval dynamics of modern Hopfield models from the fine-grained complexity analysis. Our key contribution is the characterization of a phase transition behavior in the efficiency of all possible modern Hopfield models based on the norm of patterns. Specifically, we establish an upper bound criterion for the norm of input query patterns and memory patterns. Only below this criterion, sub-quadratic (efficient) variants of the modern Hopfield model exist, assuming the Strong Exponential Time Hypothesis (SETH). To showcase our theory, we provide a formal example of efficient constructions of modern Hopfield models using low-rank approximation when the efficient criterion holds. This includes a derivation of a lower bound on the computational time, scaling linearly with $\max\{$# of stored memory patterns, length of input query sequence$\}$. In addition, we prove its memory retrieval error bound and exponential memory capacity.

[No.110] Scaling Laws for Fine-Grained Mixture of Experts
来源: ICML 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 细粒度专家混合的缩放定律
类别: 
作者: Jan Ludziejewski;Jakub Krajewski;Kamil Adamczewski;Maciej Pióro;Michał Krutul;Szymon Antoniak;Kamil Ciebiera;Krystian Król;Tomasz Odrzygóźdź;Piotr Sankowski;Marek Cygan;Sebastian Jaszczur;
机构: University of Warsaw;IDEAS NCBR;Institute for Development, Economic Analysis, and Simulation (IDEAS);Nomagic;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2024/poster/32651
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.07871
英摘: Mixture of Experts (MoE) models have emerged as a primary solution for reducing the computational cost of Large Language Models. In this work, we analyze their scaling properties, incorporating an expanded range of variables. Specifically, we introduce a new hyperparameter, granularity, whose adjustment enables precise control over the size of the experts. Building on this, we establish scaling laws for fine-grained MoE, taking into account the number of training tokens, model size, and granularity. Leveraging these laws, we derive the optimal training configuration for a given computational budget. Our findings not only show that MoE models consistently outperform dense Transformers but also highlight that the efficiency gap between dense and MoE models widens as we scale up the model size and training budget. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the common practice of setting the size of experts in MoE to mirror the feed-forward layer is not optimal at almost any computational budget.
中摘:

[No.113] Understanding Incremental Learning of Gradient Descent: A Fine-grained Analysis of Matrix Sensing
来源: ICML 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 理解梯度下降的增量学习：矩阵感知的细粒度分析
类别: 
作者: Jikai Jin;Zhiyuan Li;Kaifeng Lyu;Simon Shaolei Du;Jason D. Lee;
机构: Peking University;Stanford University;Princeton University;Meta;
类型: Poster
链接: https://icml.cc/virtual/2023/poster/25022
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2301.11500
英摘: It is believed that Gradient Descent (GD) induces an implicit bias towards good generalization in training machine learning models. This paper provides a fine-grained analysis of the dynamics of GD for the matrix sensing problem, whose goal is to recover a low-rank ground-truth matrix from near-isotropic linear measurements. It is shown that GD with small initialization behaves similarly to the greedy low-rank learning heuristics (Li et al., 2020) and follows an incremental learning procedure (Gissin et al., 2019): GD sequentially learns solutions with increasing ranks until it recovers the ground truth matrix. Compared to existing works which only analyze the first learning phase for rank-1 solutions, our result provides characterizations for the whole learning process. Moreover, besides the over-parameterized regime that many prior works focused on, our analysis of the incremental learning procedure also applies to the under-parameterized regime. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to confirm our theoretical findings.
中摘: 

一般认为，梯度下降（GD）在训练机器学习模型时会产生一种倾向于良好泛化的隐式偏差。本文对矩阵感知问题中梯度下降（GD）的动力学进行了细粒度分析，该问题的目标是从近各向同性线性测量中恢复低秩真实矩阵。研究表明，采用小初始化的梯度下降（GD）的行为与贪心低秩学习启发式方法（Li et al., 2020）相似，并遵循一种增量学习过程（Gissin et al., 2019）：GD 依次学习秩递增的解，直到恢复真实矩阵。与仅分析秩 1 解初始学习阶段的现有工作相比，我们的结果提供了整个学习过程的特征刻画。此外，除了许多先前工作关注的过参数化情形外，我们对增量学习过程的分析也适用于欠参数化情形。最后，我们通过数值实验验证了我们的理论发现。

[No.116] Beyond Scaling: Predicting Patent Approval with Domain-specific Fine-grained Claim Dependency Graph
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 超越扩展：基于领域特定细粒度权利要求依赖图的专利审批预测
类别: 
作者: Xiaochen Gao;Feng Yao;Kewen Zhao;Beilei He;Animesh Kumar;Vish Krishnan;Jingbo Shang;

英摘: Model scaling is becoming the default choice for many language tasks due to the success of large language models (LLMs). Specifically, we first extend the embedding-based state-of-the-art (SOTA) by scaling up its backbone model with various sizes of open-source LLMs, then explore prompt-based methods to harness proprietary LLMs' potential, but find the best results close to random guessing, underlining the ineffectiveness of model scaling-up. Extensive experiments and detailed analyses prove that incorporating FLAN Graph via various graph models consistently outperforms all LLM baselines significantly. We hope that our observations and analyses in this paper can bring more attention to this challenging task and prompt further research into the limitations of LLMs. Our source code and dataset can be obtained from http://github.com/ShangDataLab/FLAN-Graph.
中摘: 

由于大型语言模型（LLM）的成功，模型扩展已成为许多语言任务的默认选择。然而，在某些特定场景下，简单的定制方法表现更为出色，而模型扩展则存在不足。在本文中，我们深入研究了专利批准预测任务，并揭示出利用专利数据中的内在依赖关系，简单的领域特定图方法优于扩大模型规模。具体而言，我们首先通过利用不同规模的开源大型语言模型扩展基于嵌入的最先进（SOTA）方法的骨干模型，随后探索基于提示的方法以利用专有大型语言模型的潜力，但发现最佳结果接近随机猜测，这突显了模型扩展的无效性。因此，我们通过细致的专利数据分析，提出了一种新颖的细粒度陈述依赖（FLAN）图，以捕捉专利文本各片段之间的固有依赖关系。由于该方法具有模型无关性，我们将成本效益高的图模型应用于 FLAN 图，以获得用于批准预测的表征。广泛的实验和详细分析证明，通过各种图模型结合 FLAN 图，始终显著优于所有大型语言模型基线。我们希望本文中的观察与分析能引起人们对这一挑战性任务的更多关注，并推动对大型语言模型局限性的进一步研究。我们的源代码和数据集可从 http://github.com/ShangDataLab/FLAN-Graph 获取。

[No.120] Fine-Grained Modeling of Narrative Context: A Coherence Perspective via Retrospective Questions

[No.121] FineSurE: Fine-grained Summarization Evaluation using LLMs
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: FineSurE：使用大语言模型进行细粒度摘要评估
类别: 
作者: Hwanjun Song;Hang Su;Igor Shalyminov;Jason Cai;Saab Mansour;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Amazon;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.51/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.00908
英摘: Automated evaluation is crucial for streamlining text summarization benchmarking and model development, given the costly and time-consuming nature of human evaluation. Traditional methods like ROUGE do not correlate well with human judgment, while recently proposed LLM-based metrics provide only summary-level assessment using Likert-scale scores. This limits deeper model analysis, e.g., we can only assign one hallucination score at the summary level, while at the sentence level, we can count sentences containing hallucinations. To remedy those limitations, we propose FineSurE, a fine-grained evaluator specifically tailored for the summarization task using large language models (LLMs). It also employs completeness and conciseness criteria, in addition to faithfulness, enabling multi-dimensional assessment. We compare various open-source and proprietary LLMs as backbones for FineSurE. In addition, we conduct extensive benchmarking of FineSurE against SOTA methods including NLI-, QA-, and LLM-based methods, showing improved performance especially on the completeness and conciseness dimensions. The code is available at https://github.com/DISL-Lab/FineSurE-ACL24.
中摘: 

鉴于人工评估具有成本高且耗时的特点，自动评估对于优化文本摘要基准测试及模型开发流程至关重要。像 ROUGE 这样的传统方法与人类主观判断的相关性不高，而最近提出的基于大语言模型（LLM）的指标仅使用李克特量表评分提供摘要级别的评估。这限制了对模型的深入分析，例如，我们只能在摘要层面分配一个幻觉分数，而在句子层面，我们可以统计包含幻觉的句子数量。为弥补这些局限性，我们提出了 FineSurE，这是一个利用大语言模型（LLM）专门为摘要任务量身定制的细粒度评估器。除了忠实度之外，它还采用了完整性和简洁性标准，从而实现了多维度评估。我们比较了多种开源和专有大语言模型作为 FineSurE 的骨干模型。此外，我们将 FineSurE 与包括基于 NLI、QA 和 LLM 在内的最先进（SOTA）方法进行了广泛的基准测试，结果显示其性能有所提升，特别是在完整性和简洁性维度上。代码可在 https://github.com/DISL-Lab/FineSurE-ACL24 获取。

[No.122] FollowBench: A Multi-level Fine-grained Constraints Following Benchmark for Large Language Models
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: FollowBench：面向大型语言模型的多级细粒度约束遵循基准测试
类别: 
作者: Yuxin Jiang;Yufei Wang;Xingshan Zeng;Wanjun Zhong;Liangyou Li;Fei Mi;Lifeng Shang;Xin Jiang;Qun Liu;Wei Wang;
机构: Hong Kong University of Science and Technology;Huawei;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.257/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.20410
英摘: The ability to follow instructions is crucial for Large Language Models (LLMs) to handle various real-world applications. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on evaluating pure response quality, rather than assessing whether the response follows constraints stated in the instruction. To fill this research gap, in this paper, we propose FollowBench, a Multi-level Fine-grained Constraints Following Benchmark for LLMs. FollowBench comprehensively includes five different types (i.e., Content, Situation, Style, Format, and Example) of fine-grained constraints. To enable a precise constraint following estimation on diverse difficulties, we introduce a Multi-level mechanism that incrementally adds a single constraint to the initial instruction at each increased level. To assess whether LLMs' outputs have satisfied every individual constraint, we propose to prompt strong LLMs with constraint-evolution paths to handle challenging open-ended instructions. By evaluating 13 closed-source and open-source popular LLMs on FollowBench, we highlight the weaknesses of LLMs in instruction following and point towards potential avenues for future work. The data and code are publicly available at https://github.com/YJiangcm/FollowBench.
中摘: 

遵循指令的能力对于大型语言模型（LLMs）处理各种现实世界的应用至关重要。现有的基准测试主要侧重于评估纯粹的响应质量，而非评估响应是否遵循了指令中声明的约束。为填补这一研究空白，本文提出了 FollowBench，这是一个面向大型语言模型的多层级细粒度约束遵循基准。FollowBench 全面涵盖了五种不同类型的细粒度约束（即内容、情境、风格、格式和示例）。为了在不同难度下实现精确的约束遵循评估，我们引入了一种多层级机制，即在每一个递增的层级上逐步向初始指令添加单个约束。为了评估大型语言模型的输出是否满足了每一个单独的约束，我们提出使用约束演化路径提示强大的大型语言模型，以处理具有挑战性的开放性指令。通过在 FollowBench 上评估 13 个流行的闭源和开源大型语言模型，我们揭示了大型语言模型在指令遵循方面的弱点，并指出了未来工作的潜在方向。数据和代码可公开获取于 https://github.com/YJiangcm/FollowBench。

[No.123] How to Handle Different Types of Out-of-Distribution Scenarios in Computational Argumentation? A Comprehensive and Fine-Grained Field Study
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 如何处理不同类型的计算论证离分布场景？一项全面细致的领域研究
类别: 
作者: Andreas Waldis;Yufang Hou;Iryna Gurevych;
机构: Technical University of Darmstadt;Lucerne University of Applied Sciences and Arts;IBM;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.795/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.08316
英摘: The advent of pre-trained Language Models (LMs) has markedly advanced natural language processing, but their efficacy in out-of-distribution (OOD) scenarios remains a significant challenge. Computational argumentation (CA), modeling human argumentation processes, is a field notably impacted by these challenges because complex annotation schemes and high annotation costs naturally lead to resources barely covering the multiplicity of available text sources and topics. Due to this data scarcity, generalization to data from uncovered covariant distributions is a common challenge for CA tasks like stance detection or argument classification. This work systematically assesses LMs' capabilities for such OOD scenarios. While previous work targets specific OOD types like topic shifts or OOD uniformly, we address three prevalent OOD scenarios in CA: topic shift, domain shift, and language shift. Our findings challenge the previously asserted general superiority of in-context learning (ICL) for OOD. We find that the efficacy of such learning paradigms varies with the type of OOD. Specifically, while ICL excels for domain shifts, prompt-based fine-tuning surpasses for topic shifts. To sum up, we navigate the heterogeneity of OOD scenarios in CA and empirically underscore the potential of base-sized LMs in overcoming these challenges.
中摘: 

预训练语言模型（LMs）的出现显著推动了自然语言处理的发展，但其在分布外（OOD）场景下的有效性仍然是一个重大挑战。计算论证（CA），即对人类论证过程的建模，是受这些挑战影响尤为显著的领域，因为复杂的标注方案和高昂的标注成本导致资源难以充分覆盖可用文本来源及主题的多样性。由于这种数据稀缺性，泛化至未覆盖的协变分布数据是立场检测或论证分类等 CA 任务的常见挑战。本文系统评估了 LMs 处理此类 OOD 场景的能力。尽管以往的工作针对特定的 OOD 类型（如主题偏移）或统一的 OOD，但我们探讨了 CA 中三个常见的 OOD 场景：主题偏移、领域偏移和语言偏移。我们的研究结果挑战了此前关于上下文学习（ICL）在 OOD 方面具有普遍优越性的断言。我们发现，此类学习范式的效能随 OOD 类型的不同而有所差异。具体而言，虽然 ICL 在领域偏移方面表现出色，但在主题偏移方面，基于提示的微调则表现更优。总之，我们探索了 CA 中 OOD 场景的异质性，并通过实证强调了基座规模 LMs 在克服这些挑战方面的潜力。

[No.124] Improving Large Language Models via Fine-grained Reinforcement Learning with Minimum Editing Constraint
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过最小编辑约束的细粒度强化学习改进大型语言模型
类别: 
作者: Zhipeng Chen;Kun Zhou;Xin Zhao;Junchen Wan;Fuzheng Zhang;Di Zhang;Ji-Rong Wen;

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06081
英摘: Reinforcement learning (RL) has been widely used in training large language models (LLMs) for preventing unexpected outputs, eg reducing harmfulness and errors. However, existing RL methods mostly adopt the instance-level reward, which is unable to provide fine-grained supervision for complex reasoning tasks, and can not focus on the few key tokens that lead to the incorrectness. To address it, we propose a new RL method named RLMEC that incorporates a generative model as the reward model, which is trained by the erroneous solution rewriting task under the minimum editing constraint, and can produce token-level rewards for RL training. Based on the generative reward model, we design the token-level RL objective for training and an imitation-based regularization for stabilizing RL process. And the both objectives focus on the learning of the key tokens for the erroneous solution, reducing the effect of other unimportant tokens. The experiment results on mathematical tasks and question-answering tasks have demonstrated the effectiveness of our approach. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/RUCAIBox/RLMEC.
中摘: 

强化学习（RL）已被广泛应用于大型语言模型（LLMs）的训练，以防止非预期输出，例如减少有害性和错误。然而，现有的 RL 方法大多采用实例级奖励，无法为复杂推理任务提供细粒度监督，也无法关注导致错误的少数关键词元。为解决这一问题，我们提出了一种名为 RLMEC 的新型 RL 方法，该方法将生成模型作为奖励模型，该模型在最小编辑约束下的错误解重写任务上进行训练，并能生成用于 RL 训练的词元级奖励。基于该生成奖励模型，我们设计了用于训练的词元级 RL 目标函数，以及用于稳定 RL 过程的基于模仿的正则化。这两个目标均聚焦于错误解中关键词元的学习，从而降低其他不相关词元的影响。数学任务和问答任务上的实验结果证明了该方法的有效性。我们的代码和数据可在 https://github.com/RUCAIBox/RLMEC 获取。

[No.126] Integrate the Essence and Eliminate the Dross: Fine-Grained Self-Consistency for Free-Form Language Generation
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 去粗取精，精细自洽：自由形式语言生成中的细粒度自我一致性
类别: 
作者: Xinglin Wang;Yiwei Li;Shaoxiong Feng;Peiwen Yuan;Boyuan Pan;Heda Wang;Yao Hu;Kan Li;
机构: Beijing Institute of Technology;Xiaohongshu Inc;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.634/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02056
英摘: Self-consistency (SC), leveraging multiple samples from LLMs, shows significant gains on various reasoning tasks but struggles with free-form generation due to the difficulty of aggregating answers. Its variants, UCS and USC, rely on sample selection or voting mechanisms to improve output quality. These methods, however, face limitations due to their inability to fully utilize the nuanced consensus knowledge present within multiple candidate samples, often resulting in suboptimal outputs. We propose Fine-Grained Self-Consistency (FSC) to addresses these limitations by extracting and integrating segment-level commonalities from candidate samples, enhancing the performance of LLMs both in open-ended and reasoning tasks. Based on this, we present two additional strategies: candidate filtering, which enhances overall quality by identifying highly similar candidate sets, and merging, which reduces input token requirements by combining similar samples. The effectiveness of FSC is demonstrated through extensive experiments on various tasks, including summarization, code generation, and mathematical reasoning, using GPT-3.5-turbo and GPT-4. The results indicate significant improvements over baseline methods, showcasing the potential of FSC to optimize output quality by effectively synthesizing fine-grained consensus knowledge from multiple samples.
中摘: 

自一致性（SC）利用来自大语言模型（LLM）的多个样本，在各种推理任务上取得了显著成效，但由于答案聚合困难，在自由形式生成方面面临挑战。其变体 UCS 和 USC 依赖样本选择或投票机制来提升输出质量。然而，这些方法因无法充分利用多个候选样本中存在的细微共识知识而面临局限性，通常导致输出结果次优。为此，我们提出了细粒度自一致性（FSC），通过从候选样本中提取并整合片段级共性来解决上述局限，增强了大语言模型在开放型及推理任务中的性能。基于此，我们提出了两种额外策略：候选过滤，通过识别高度相似的候选集来提升整体质量；以及合并，通过组合相似样本来减少输入 token 的需求。通过在 GPT-3.5-turbo 和 GPT-4 上对包括摘要、代码生成和数学推理在内的各项任务进行广泛实验，验证了 FSC 的有效性。结果表明，与基线方法相比有显著提升，展现了 FSC 通过有效综合多个样本中的细粒度共识知识来优化输出质量的潜力。

[No.127] KPEval: Towards Fine-Grained Semantic-Based Keyphrase Evaluation
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 基于细粒度语义的关键词评价研究
类别: 
作者: Di Wu;Da Yin;Kai-Wei Chang;
机构: University of California, Los Angeles;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.117/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.15422
英摘: Despite the significant advancements in keyphrase extraction and keyphrase generation methods, the predominant approach for evaluation mainly relies on exact matching with human references. This scheme fails to recognize systems that generate keyphrases semantically equivalent to the references or diverse keyphrases that carry practical utility. To better assess the capability of keyphrase systems, we propose KPEval, a comprehensive evaluation framework consisting of four critical aspects: reference agreement, faithfulness, diversity, and utility. For each aspect, we design semantic-based metrics to reflect the evaluation objectives. Meta-evaluation studies demonstrate that our evaluation strategy correlates better with human preferences compared to a range of previously proposed metrics. Using KPEval, we re-evaluate 23 keyphrase systems and discover that (1) established model comparison results have blind-spots especially when considering reference-free evaluation; (2) large language models are underestimated by prior evaluation works; and (3) there is no single best model that can excel in all the aspects.
中摘: 

尽管关键词抽取和生成方法取得了显著进展，但主流的评估方法仍主要依赖与人工参考的精确匹配。该方案无法识别生成与参考语义等价或具有实用价值的多样化关键词的系统。为了更好地评估关键词系统的能力，我们提出了 KPEval，这是一个包含四个关键方面的综合评估框架：参考一致性、忠实性、多样性和实用性。针对每个方面，我们设计了基于语义的指标以反映评估目标。元评估研究表明，与一系列先前提出的指标相比，我们的评估策略与人类偏好具有更好的相关性。利用 KPEval，我们重新评估了 23 个关键词系统，并发现：(1) 现有的模型比较结果存在盲区，特别是在考虑无参考评估时；(2) 大语言模型被先前的评估工作低估了；(3) 没有单一模型能在所有方面均表现出色。

来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 细粒度视觉接地引文学习for带属性的大语言模型
类别: 
作者: Lei Huang;Xiaocheng Feng;Weitao Ma;Yuxuan Gu;Weihong Zhong;Xiachong Feng;Weijiang Yu;Weihua Peng;Duyu Tang;Dandan Tu;Bing Qin;

英摘: Despite the impressive performance on information-seeking tasks, large language models (LLMs) still struggle with hallucinations. Attributed LLMs, which augment generated text with in-line citations, have shown potential in mitigating hallucinations and improving verifiability. However, current approaches suffer from suboptimal citation quality due to their reliance on in-context learning. Furthermore, the practice of citing only coarse document identifiers makes it challenging for users to perform fine-grained verification. In this work, we introduce FRONT, a training framework designed to teach LLMs to generate Fine-Grained Grounded Citations. By grounding model outputs in fine-grained supporting quotes, these quotes guide the generation of grounded and consistent responses, not only improving citation quality but also facilitating fine-grained verification. Experiments on the ALCE benchmark demonstrate the efficacy of FRONT in generating superior grounded responses and highly supportive citations. 英摘: Mental manipulation, a significant form of abuse in interpersonal conversations, presents a challenge to identify due to its context-dependent and often subtle nature. Our study addresses this gap by introducing a new dataset, named ${\rm M{\small ental}M{\small anip}}$, which consists of $4,000$ annotated movie dialogues. [No.130] MIKE: A New Benchmark for Fine-grained Multimodal Entity Knowledge Editing
来源: ACL 2024

标题: MIKE：细粒度多模态实体知识编辑的新基准

英摘: Multimodal knowledge editing represents a critical advancement in enhancing the capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). [No.131] MT-Bench-101: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Models in Multi-Turn Dialogues
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: MT-Bench-101：一个细粒度的多轮对话大型语言模型评估基准
类别: 
作者: Ge Bai;Jie Liu;Xingyuan Bu;Yancheng He;Jiaheng Liu;Zhanhui Zhou;Zhuoran Lin;Wenbo Su;Tiezheng Ge;Bo Zheng;Wanli Ouyang;
机构: Alibaba Group;Chinese University of Hong Kong;Shanghai AI Laboratory;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.401/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.14762
英摘: The advent of Large Language Models (LLMs) has drastically enhanced dialogue systems. However, comprehensively evaluating the dialogue abilities of LLMs remains a challenge. Previous benchmarks have primarily focused on single-turn dialogues or provided coarse-grained and incomplete assessments of multi-turn dialogues, overlooking the complexity and fine-grained nuances of real-life dialogues. To address this issue, we introduce MT-Bench-101, specifically designed to evaluate the fine-grained abilities of LLMs in multi-turn dialogues. By conducting a detailed analysis of real multi-turn dialogue data, we construct a three-tier hierarchical ability taxonomy comprising 4208 turns across 1388 multi-turn dialogues in 13 distinct tasks. We then evaluate 21 popular LLMs based on MT-Bench-101, conducting comprehensive analyses from both ability and task perspectives and observing differing trends in LLMs performance across dialogue turns within various tasks. Further analysis indicates that neither utilizing common alignment techniques nor chat-specific designs has led to obvious enhancements in the multi-turn abilities of LLMs. Extensive case studies suggest that our designed tasks accurately assess the corresponding multi-turn abilities. The data and code are available at \url{https://github.com/mtbench101/mt-bench-101}.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLMs）的出现极大地增强了对话系统。然而，全面评估 LLMs 的对话能力仍然是一项挑战。现有的基准测试主要聚焦于单轮对话，或对多轮对话提供了粗粒度且不完整的评估，忽视了真实对话的复杂性和细粒度细微差别。为解决这一问题，我们提出了 MT-Bench-101，专门设计用于评估 LLMs 在多轮对话中的细粒度能力。通过对真实多轮对话数据进行详细分析，我们构建了一个三层层次化的能力分类体系，涵盖 13 个不同任务下的 1388 个多轮对话，共计 4208 轮。随后，我们基于 MT-Bench-101 对 21 个流行的 LLMs 进行了评估，从能力和任务两个视角进行了综合分析，并观察到不同任务中 LLMs 的性能在对话轮次间呈现出不同的趋势。进一步分析表明，无论是采用常见的对齐技术还是针对聊天的特定设计，均未带来 LLMs 多轮对话能力的显著提升。大量案例研究证实，我们设计的任务能够准确评估相应的多轮对话能力。数据和代码可在 \url{https://github.com/mtbench101/mt-bench-101} 获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.132] PreFLMR: Scaling Up Fine-Grained Late-Interaction Multi-modal Retrievers
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: PreFLMR: 扩展细粒度延迟交互多模态检索器
类别: 
作者: Weizhe Lin;Jingbiao Mei;Jinghong Chen;Bill Byrne;
机构: University of Cambridge;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.289/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.08327
英摘: Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) excel in natural language and visual understanding but are challenged by exacting tasks such as Knowledge-based Visual Question Answering (KB-VQA) which involve the retrieval of relevant information from document collections to use in shaping answers to questions. We present an extensive training and evaluation framework, M2KR, for KB-VQA. M2KR contains a collection of vision and language tasks which we have incorporated into a single suite of benchmark tasks for training and evaluating general-purpose multi-modal retrievers. We use M2KR to develop PreFLMR, a pre-trained version of the recently developed Fine-grained Late-interaction Multi-modal Retriever (FLMR) approach to KB-VQA, and we report new state-of-the-art results across a range of tasks. We also present investigations into the scaling behaviors of PreFLMR intended to be useful in future developments in general-purpose multi-modal retrievers.
中摘: 

大型多模态模型（LMMs）在自然语言和视觉理解方面表现卓越，但在处理如基于知识的视觉问答（KB-VQA）等严苛任务时面临挑战，此类任务涉及从文档集合中检索相关信息以构建回答。我们提出了一个用于 KB-VQA 的广泛训练与评估框架 M2KR。M2KR 包含一系列视觉和语言任务，我们将其整合为一个统一的基准任务集，用于训练和评估通用多模态检索器。利用 M2KR，我们开发了 PreFLMR，这是近期提出的用于 KB-VQA 的细粒度晚期交互多模态检索器（FLMR）方法的预训练版本，我们在多种任务上取得了新的最先进成果。此外，我们还针对 PreFLMR 的缩放行为进行了研究，旨在为未来通用多模态检索器的发展提供参考。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.133] Prometheus-Vision: Vision-Language Model as a Judge for Fine-Grained Evaluation
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: Prometheus-Vision：视觉语言模型作为细粒度评估的裁判
类别: 
作者: Seongyun Lee;Seungone Kim;Sue Park;Geewook Kim;Minjoon Seo;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Carnegie Mellon University;NAVER Corporation;NAVER Cloud;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.672/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2401.06591
英摘: Assessing long-form responses generated by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is challenging. It not only requires checking whether the VLM follows the given instruction but also verifying whether the text output is properly grounded on the given image. Inspired by the recent approach of evaluating LMs with LMs, in this work, we propose to evaluate VLMs with VLMs. For this purpose, we present a new feedback dataset called the Perception Collection, encompassing 15K customized score rubrics that users might care about during assessment. Using the Perception Collection, we train Prometheus-Vision, the first open-source VLM evaluator model that can understand the user-defined score criteria during evaluation. Prometheus-Vision shows the highest Pearson correlation with human evaluators and GPT-4V among open-source models, showing its effectiveness for transparent and accessible evaluation of VLMs. We open-source our code, dataset, and model at https://github.com/kaistAI/prometheus-vision
中摘: 

评估由视觉 - 语言模型（VLMs）生成的长文本响应具有挑战性。这不仅需要检查 VLM 是否遵循了给定指令，还需要验证文本输出是否恰当地基于给定图像。受近期利用语言模型（LMs）评估语言模型这一方法的启发，在本研究中，我们提出利用视觉 - 语言模型（VLMs）来评估 VLMs。为此，我们提出了一个新的反馈数据集，称为 Perception Collection，其中包含 1.5 万个在评估过程中用户可能关心的定制化评分标准。利用 Perception Collection，我们训练了 Prometheus-Vision，这是首个能够在评估过程中理解用户定义评分标准的开源 VLM 评估模型。在开源模型中，Prometheus-Vision 展现出与人类评估者及 GPT-4V 最高的皮尔逊相关系数，证明了其在实现 VLMs 透明且易于获取的评估方面的有效性。我们将代码、数据集和模型开源在 https://github.com/kaistAI/prometheus-vision。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.134] TLCR: Token-Level Continuous Reward for Fine-grained Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 基于令牌级连续奖励的细粒度强化学习（从人类反馈）
类别: 
作者: Eunseop Yoon;Hee Suk Yoon;SooHwan Eom;Gunsoo Han;Daniel Nam;Daejin Jo;Kyoung-Woon On;Mark Hasegawa-Johnson;Sungwoong Kim;Chang Yoo;
机构: Korea Advanced Institute of Science and Technology;Kakao Brain;Korea University;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.889/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.16574
英摘: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) leverages human preference data to train language models to align more closely with human essence. These human preference data, however, are labeled at the sequence level, creating a mismatch between sequence-level preference labels and tokens, which are autoregressively generated from the language model. Although several recent approaches have tried to provide token-level (i.e., dense) rewards for each individual token, these typically rely on predefined discrete reward values (e.g., positive: +1, negative: -1, neutral: 0), failing to account for varying degrees of preference inherent to each token. To address this limitation, we introduce TLCR (Token-Level Continuous Reward) for RLHF, which incorporates a discriminator trained to distinguish positive and negative tokens, and the confidence of the discriminator is used to assign continuous rewards to each token considering the context. Extensive experiments show that our proposed TLCR leads to consistent performance improvements over previous sequence-level or token-level discrete rewards on open-ended generation benchmarks.
中摘: 

来自人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）利用人类偏好数据训练语言模型，使其更紧密地契合人类本质。然而，这些人类偏好数据通常在序列级进行标注，导致序列级偏好标签与由语言模型自回归生成的词元之间存在不匹配。尽管近期一些方法尝试为每个独立词元提供词元级（即稠密）奖励，但这些方法通常依赖于预定义的离散奖励值（例如：正：+1，负：-1，中性：0），未能考虑到每个词元固有的不同程度的偏好。为解决这一局限，我们引入了用于 RLHF 的 TLCR（词元级连续奖励），该方法包含一个经过训练以区分正负词元的判别器，并利用判别器的置信度结合上下文为每个词元分配连续奖励。大量实验表明，我们提出的 TLCR 在开放生成基准上，相较于以往的序列级或词元级离散奖励，带来了持续的性能提升。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.135] Training Language Models to Generate Text with Citations via Fine-grained Rewards
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过细粒度奖励训练语言模型生成带有引用的文字
类别: 
作者: Chengyu Huang;Zeqiu Wu;Yushi Hu;Wenya Wang;
机构: National University of Singapore;University of Washington;Nanyang Technological University;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.acl-long.161/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.04315
英摘: While recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have proven useful in answering user queries, they are prone to hallucination, and their responses often lack credibility due to missing references to reliable sources. An intuitive solution to these issues would be to include in-text citations referring to external documents as evidence. While previous works have directly prompted LLMs to generate in-text citations, their performances are far from satisfactory, especially when it comes to smaller LLMs. In this work, we propose an effective training framework using fine-grained rewards to teach LLMs to generate highly supportive and relevant citations, while ensuring the correctness of their responses. We also conduct a systematic analysis of applying these fine-grained rewards to common LLM training strategies, demonstrating its advantage over conventional practices. We conduct extensive experiments on Question Answering (QA) datasets taken from the ALCE benchmark and validate the model's generalizability using EXPERTQA. On LLaMA-2-7B, the incorporation of fine-grained rewards achieves the best performance among the baselines, even surpassing that of GPT-3.5-turbo.
中摘: 

尽管近期的大型语言模型（LLMs）在回答用户查询方面已显示出实用性，但它们容易产生幻觉，且因缺乏对可靠来源的引证，其回复往往缺乏可信度。解决这些问题的一个直观方案是加入引用外部文档作为证据的文内引注。尽管以往的研究曾直接提示大型语言模型生成文内引注，但其表现远未达到令人满意的程度，尤其是针对较小规模的语言模型而言。在这项工作中，我们提出了一个有效的训练框架，利用细粒度奖励教导大型语言模型生成高度支持性且相关的引注，同时确保其回答的正确性。我们还系统地分析了将此类细粒度奖励应用于常见大型语言模型训练策略的效果，证明了其相较于传统实践的优势。我们在取自 ALCE 基准的问答（QA）数据集上进行了广泛的实验，并使用 EXPERTQA 验证了模型的泛化能力。在 LLaMA-2-7B 模型上，引入细粒度奖励在基线模型中实现了最佳性能，甚至超越了 GPT-3.5-turbo。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.136] Understanding Fine-grained Distortions in Reports of Scientific Findings
来源: ACL 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 理解科学发现报告中的细微偏差
类别: 
作者: Amelie Wuehrl;Dustin Wright;Roman Klinger;Isabelle Augenstein;
机构: University of Stuttgart;University of Bamberg;University of Copenhagen;
类型: Findings
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-acl.369/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.12431
英摘: Distorted science communication harms individuals and society as it can lead to unhealthy behavior change and decrease trust in scientific institutions. Given the rapidly increasing volume of science communication in recent years, a fine-grained understanding of how findings from scientific publications are reported to the general public, and methods to detect distortions from the original work automatically, are crucial. Prior work focused on individual aspects of distortions or worked with unpaired data. In this work, we make three foundational contributions towards addressing this problem: (1) annotating 1,600 instances of scientific findings from academic papers paired with corresponding findings as reported in news articles and tweets wrt. four characteristics: causality, certainty, generality and sensationalism; (2) establishing baselines for automatically detecting these characteristics; and (3) analyzing the prevalence of changes in these characteristics in both human-annotated and large-scale unlabeled data. Our results show that scientific findings frequently undergo subtle distortions when reported. Tweets distort findings more often than science news reports. Detecting fine-grained distortions automatically poses a challenging task. In our experiments, fine-tuned task-specific models consistently outperform few-shot LLM prompting.
中摘: 

失真的科学传播会危害个人和社会，因为它可能导致不健康的行为改变并降低对科学机构的信任。鉴于近年来科学传播量迅速增长，对科学出版物中的发现如何向公众进行报道进行细粒度理解，以及自动检测这些发现与原始研究之间失真的方法，都至关重要。先前的工作要么专注于失真的个别方面，要么使用未配对数据。在本工作中，我们为解决这一问题做出了三项基础性贡献：(1) 标注了 1,600 个科学发现实例，将学术论文中的发现与新闻报道和推文中的对应发现进行配对，涉及四个特征：因果关系、确定性、普遍性和煽情性；(2) 建立自动检测这些特征的基线；(3) 分析人工标注和大规模未标注数据中这些特征变化的普遍性。我们的结果表明，科学发现在报道时经常经历细微的失真。推文对发现的扭曲比科学新闻报道更为频繁。自动检测细粒度的失真是一项具有挑战性的任务。在我们的实验中，微调的特定任务模型始终优于少样本大语言模型提示。

[No.143] Facilitating Fine-grained Detection of Chinese Toxic Language: Hierarchical Taxonomy, Resources, and Benchmarks
来源: ACL 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 促进细粒度检测中文有毒语言：层次分类体系、资源和基准
类别: 
作者: Junyu Lu;Bo Xu;Xiaokun Zhang;Changrong Min;Liang Yang;Hongfei Lin;
机构: Dalian University of Technology;
类型: Long
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2023.acl-long.898/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.04446
英摘: The widespread dissemination of toxic online posts is increasingly damaging to society. However, research on detecting toxic language in Chinese has lagged significantly. Existing datasets lack fine-grained annotation of toxic types and expressions, and ignore the samples with indirect toxicity. In addition, it is crucial to introduce lexical knowledge to detect the toxicity of posts, which has been a challenge for researchers. In this paper, we facilitate the fine-grained detection of Chinese toxic language. First, we built Monitor Toxic Frame, a hierarchical taxonomy to analyze toxic types and expressions. Then, a fine-grained dataset ToxiCN is presented, including both direct and indirect toxic samples. We also build an insult lexicon containing implicit profanity and propose Toxic Knowledge Enhancement (TKE) as a benchmark, incorporating the lexical feature to detect toxic language. In the experimental stage, we demonstrate the effectiveness of TKE. After that, a systematic quantitative and qualitative analysis of the findings is given.
中摘: 

有毒网络帖子的广泛传播正日益对社会造成危害。然而，中文有毒语言检测的研究工作严重滞后。现有数据集缺乏对有毒类型及表达方式的细粒度标注，且忽视了具有间接毒性的样本。此外，引入词汇知识以检测帖子毒性至关重要，但这一直是研究人员面临的挑战。本文致力于推动中文有毒语言的细粒度检测。首先，我们构建了监控有毒框架（Monitor Toxic Frame），这是一种用于分析有毒类型和表达方式的层级分类体系。随后，我们提出了一个细粒度数据集 ToxiCN，其中包含直接和间接有毒样本。此外，我们还构建了一个包含隐性粗口的辱骂词典，并提出了有毒知识增强（Toxic Knowledge Enhancement, TKE）作为基准，通过整合词汇特征来检测有毒语言。在实验阶段，我们验证了 TKE 的有效性。最后，我们对研究结果进行了系统的定量和定性分析。

[No.149] MISMATCH: Fine-grained Evaluation of Machine-generated Text with Mismatch Error Types
来源: ACL 2023
命中: fine-grained

[No.159] EFUF: Efficient Fine-Grained Unlearning Framework for Mitigating Hallucinations in Multimodal Large Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 高效细粒度遗忘框架：减轻多模态大型语言模型幻觉问题
类别: 
作者: Shangyu Xing;Fei Zhao;Zhen Wu;Tuo An;Weihao Chen;Chunhui Li;Jianbing Zhang;Xinyu Dai;
机构: Nanjing University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.67/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.09801
英摘: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have attracted increasing attention in the past few years, but they may still generate descriptions that include objects not present in the corresponding images, a phenomenon known as object hallucination. To eliminate hallucinations, existing methods manually annotate paired responses with and without hallucinations, and then employ various alignment algorithms to improve the alignment capability between images and text. However, they not only demand considerable computation resources during the finetuning stage but also require expensive human annotation to construct paired data needed by the alignment algorithms. To address these issues, we borrow the idea of unlearning and propose an efficient fine-grained unlearning framework (EFUF), which can eliminate hallucinations without the need for paired data. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently reduces hallucinations while preserving the generation quality with modest computational overhead. Our code and datasets will be publicly available.
中摘: 

多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在过去几年中吸引了越来越多的关注，但它们仍可能生成包含对应图像中不存在物体的描述，这种现象被称为物体幻觉。为了消除幻觉，现有方法通常手动标注包含和不包含幻觉的成对响应，随后采用各种对齐算法来提升图像与文本之间的对齐能力。然而，这些方法不仅在微调阶段需要消耗大量计算资源，还需要昂贵的人工标注来构建对齐算法所需的配对数据。为解决这些问题，我们借鉴了“遗忘”的理念，提出了一种高效的细粒度遗忘框架（EFUF），该框架能够在无需配对数据的情况下消除幻觉。大量实验表明，我们的方法在保持生成质量的同时能持续减少幻觉，且计算开销适中。我们的代码和数据集将公开可用。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.160] EmoKnob: Enhance Voice Cloning with Fine-Grained Emotion Control
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: EmoKnob：细粒度情感控制增强语音克隆
类别: 
作者: Haozhe Chen;Run Chen;Julia Hirschberg;
机构: Columbia University;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.466/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.00316
英摘: While recent advances in Text-to-Speech (TTS) technology produce natural and expressive speech, they lack the option for users to select emotion and control intensity. We propose EmoKnob, a framework that allows fine-grained emotion control in speech synthesis with few-shot demonstrative samples of arbitrary emotion. Our framework leverages the expressive speaker representation space made possible by recent advances in foundation voice cloning models. Based on the few-shot capability of our emotion control framework, we propose two methods to apply emotion control on emotions described by open-ended text, enabling an intuitive interface for controlling a diverse array of nuanced emotions. To facilitate a more systematic emotional speech synthesis field, we introduce a set of evaluation metrics designed to rigorously assess the faithfulness and recognizability of emotion control frameworks. Through objective and subjective evaluations, we show that our emotion control framework effectively embeds emotions into speech and surpasses emotion expressiveness of commercial TTS services.
中摘: 

尽管近期文本转语音（TTS）技术的进步能够生成自然且富有表现力的语音，但它们缺乏让用户选择情感并控制强度的选项。我们提出了 EmoKnob，这是一个允许在语音合成中利用任意情感的少样本演示样本实现细粒度情感控制的框架。我们的框架利用了由近期基础语音克隆模型的进步所实现的富有表现力的说话人表征空间。基于我们情感控制框架的少样本能力，我们提出了两种方法，用于对由开放式文本描述的情感进行控制，从而实现了能够操控多样化微妙情感的直观界面。为了促进情感语音合成领域更加系统化，我们引入了一套评估指标，旨在严格评估情感控制框架的忠实度和可识别性。通过客观和主观评估，我们证明我们的情感控制框架能够有效地将情感融入语音中，并超越了商业 TTS 服务的情感表达力。

[No.161] Enhancing Fine-Grained Image Classifications via Cascaded Vision Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过级联视觉语言模型增强细粒度图像分类
类别: 
作者: Canshi Wei;
机构: Tencent;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.102/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.11301
英摘: Fine-grained image classification, particularly in zero/few-shot scenarios, presents a significant challenge for vision-language models (VLMs), such as CLIP. These models often struggle with the nuanced task of distinguishing between semantically similar classes due to limitations in their pre-trained recipe, which lacks supervision signals for fine-grained categorization. This paper introduces CascadeVLM, an innovative framework that overcomes the constraints of previous CLIP-based methods by effectively leveraging the granular knowledge encapsulated within large vision-language models (LVLMs). Experiments across various fine-grained image datasets demonstrate that CascadeVLM significantly outperforms existing models, specifically on the Stanford Cars dataset, achieving an impressive 85.6% zero-shot accuracy. Performance gain analysis validates that LVLMs produce more accurate predictions for challenging images that CLIPs are uncertain about, bringing the overall accuracy boost. Our framework sheds light on a holistic integration of VLMs and LVLMs for effective and efficient fine-grained image classification.
中摘: 

细粒度图像分类，特别是在零样本/少样本场景下，对视觉 - 语言模型（VLMs）（如 CLIP）构成了重大挑战。这些模型往往难以完成区分语义相似类别的精细任务，这是由于其预训练机制存在局限性，缺乏针对细粒度分类的监督信号。本文提出了 CascadeVLM，这是一种创新框架，通过有效利用大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）中蕴含的细粒度知识，克服了以往基于 CLIP 方法的局限性。在各种细粒度图像数据集上的实验表明，CascadeVLM 显著优于现有模型，特别是在斯坦福汽车数据集上，达到了令人印象深刻的 85.6% 零样本准确率。性能增益分析证实，LVLMs 能够针对 CLIP 模型不确定的挑战性图像提供更准确的预测，从而带来了整体准确率的提升。我们的框架为 VLM 与 LVLM 的全面融合提供了新见解，旨在实现高效且有效的细粒度图像分类。

[No.162] Exploring the Limits of Fine-grained LLM-based Physics Inference via Premise Removal Interventions
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过前提移除干预探索基于细粒度LLM的物理推理能力极限
类别: 
作者: Jordan Meadows;Tamsin Emily James;Andre Freitas;
机构: University of Manchester;Idiap Research Institute;CRUK-MI;University of Birmingham;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.378/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.18384
英摘: Language models (LMs) can hallucinate when performing complex mathematical reasoning. Physics provides a rich domain for assessing their mathematical capabilities, where physical context requires that any symbolic manipulation satisfies complex semantics (\textit{e.g.,} units, tensorial order). In this work, we systematically remove crucial context from prompts to force instances where model inference may be algebraically coherent, yet unphysical. We assess LM capabilities in this domain using a curated dataset encompassing multiple notations and Physics subdomains. Further, we improve zero-shot scores using synthetic in-context examples, and demonstrate non-linear degradation of derivation quality with perturbation strength via the progressive omission of supporting premises. We find that the models' mathematical reasoning is not physics-informed in this setting, where physical context is predominantly ignored in favour of reverse-engineering solutions.
中摘: 

语言模型（LMs）在进行复杂数学推理时会产生幻觉。物理学为评估其数学能力提供了一个丰富的领域，其中的物理背景要求任何符号操作都必须满足复杂的语义（例如，单位、张量阶次）。在这项工作中，我们系统地移除提示词中的关键上下文，以促使出现模型推理可能在代数上自洽，但却违背物理规律的情形。我们使用包含多种符号体系和物理学子领域的精选数据集，来评估该领域内语言模型的能力。此外，我们利用合成上下文示例提高了零样本得分，并通过渐进式省略支撑前提，展示了推导质量随扰动强度呈现的非线性退化。我们发现，在此设定下，模型的数学推理并非基于物理知识，物理背景往往被忽略，转而优先采用逆向工程式的方法进行求解。
