[No.163] Factcheck-Bench: Fine-Grained Evaluation Benchmark for Automatic Fact-checkers
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: Factcheck-Bench：自动事实核查器的细粒度评估基准
类别: 
作者: Yuxia Wang;Revanth Gangi Reddy;Zain Muhammad Mujahid;Arnav Arora;Aleksandr Rubashevskii;Jiahui Geng;Osama Mohammed Afzal;Liangming Pan;Nadav Borenstein;Aditya Pillai;Isabelle Augenstein;Iryna Gurevych;Preslav Nakov;
机构: Mohamed bin Zayed University of Artificial Intelligence;University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;University of Copenhagen;Skolkovo Institute of Science and Technology;University of California, Santa Barbara;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.830/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.09000
英摘: The increased use of large language models (LLMs) across a variety of real-world applications calls for mechanisms to verify the factual accuracy of their outputs. In this work, we present a holistic end-to-end solution for annotating the factuality of LLM-generated responses, which encompasses a multi-stage annotation scheme designed to yield detailed labels concerning the verifiability and factual inconsistencies found in LLM outputs. We further construct an open-domain document-level factuality benchmark in three-level granularity: claim, sentence and document, aiming to facilitate the evaluation of automatic fact-checking systems. Preliminary experiments show that FacTool, FactScore and Perplexity.ai are struggling to identify false claims, with the best F1=0.63 by this annotation solution based on GPT-4. Annotation tool, benchmark and code are available at https://github.com/yuxiaw/Factcheck-GPT.
中摘: 

大型语言模型（LLM）在各种实际应用场景中的日益普及，亟需验证其输出事实准确性的机制。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种用于标注大型语言模型生成响应事实性的整体端到端解决方案，该方案包含一个多阶段标注体系，旨在生成关于大型语言模型输出中可验证性和事实不一致性的详细标签。我们进一步构建了一个开放域文档级事实性基准，具有三个层级粒度：主张、句子和文档，旨在促进自动事实核查系统的评估。初步实验表明，FacTool、FactScore 和 Perplexity.ai 在识别虚假主张方面表现不佳，而基于 GPT-4 的此标注解决方案取得了最佳 F1 分数 0.63。标注工具、基准和代码可在 https://github.com/yuxiaw/Factcheck-GPT 获取。

[No.164] FaithScore: Fine-grained Evaluations of Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: FaithScore：大型视觉-语言模型中的细粒度幻觉评估
类别: 
作者: Liqiang Jing;Ruosen Li;Yunmo Chen;Xinya Du;
机构: University of Texas at Dallas;Johns Hopkins University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.290/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2311.01477
英摘: We introduce FaithScore (Faithfulness to Atomic Image Facts Score), a reference-free and fine-grained evaluation metric that measures the faithfulness of the generated free-form answers from large vision-language models (LVLMs). The FaithScore evaluation first identifies sub-sentences containing descriptive statements that need to be verified, then extracts a comprehensive list of atomic facts from these sub-sentences, and finally conducts consistency verification between fine-grained atomic facts and the input image. Meta-evaluation demonstrates that our metric highly correlates with human judgments of faithfulness. We collect two benchmark datasets (i.e. LLaVA-1k and MSCOCO-Cap) for evaluating LVLMs instruction-following hallucinations. We measure hallucinations in state-of-the-art LVLMs with FaithScore on the datasets. Results reveal that current systems are prone to generate hallucinated content unfaithful to the image, which leaves room for future improvements. We hope our metric FaithScore can help evaluate future LVLMs in terms of faithfulness and provide insightful advice for enhancing LVLMs' faithfulness.
中摘: 

我们提出了 FaithScore（原子图像事实忠实度评分），这是一种无参考且细粒度的评估指标，用于衡量大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）生成的自由形式回答的忠实度。FaithScore 评估首先识别包含待验证描述性陈述的子句，然后从中提取全面的原子事实列表，最后对细粒度原子事实与输入图像进行一致性验证。元评估表明，我们的指标与人类对忠实度的判断高度相关。我们收集了两个基准数据集（即 LLaVA-1k 和 MSCOCO-Cap），用于评估 LVLM 的指令遵循幻觉。我们在这些数据集上使用 FaithScore 测量了最先进的 LVLM 中的幻觉。结果显示，当前系统容易产生与图像不符的幻觉内容，这为未来的改进留下了空间。我们希望我们的指标 FaithScore 能帮助评估未来 LVLM 在忠实度方面的表现，并为增强 LVLM 的忠实度提供具有洞察力的建议。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.165] Fine-Grained Detection of Solidarity for Women and Migrants in 155 Years of German Parliamentary Debates
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 155年德国议会辩论中的女性和移民团结细粒度检测
类别: 
作者: Aida Kostikova;Dominik Beese;Benjamin Paassen;Ole Pütz;Gregor Wiedemann;Steffen Eger;
机构: Bielefeld University;Technische Universität Darmstadt;Hans-Bredow-Institut;University of Mannheim;Nuremberg University of Technology;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.337/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2210.04359
英摘: Solidarity is a crucial concept to understand social relations in societies. In this paper, we explore fine-grained solidarity frames to study solidarity towards women and migrants in German parliamentary debates between 1867 and 2022. Using 2,864 manually annotated text snippets (with a cost exceeding 18k Euro), we evaluate large language models (LLMs) like Llama 3, GPT-3.5, and GPT-4. We find that GPT-4 outperforms other LLMs, approaching human annotation quality. Using GPT-4, we automatically annotate more than 18k further instances (with a cost of around 500 Euro) across 155 years and find that solidarity with migrants outweighs anti-solidarity but that frequencies and solidarity types shift over time. Most importantly, group-based notions of (anti-)solidarity fade in favor of compassionate solidarity, focusing on the vulnerability of migrant groups, and exchange-based anti-solidarity, focusing on the lack of (economic) contribution. Our study highlights the interplay of historical events, socio-economic needs, and political ideologies in shaping migration discourse and social cohesion. We also show that powerful LLMs, if carefully prompted, can be cost-effective alternatives to human annotation for hard social scientific tasks.
中摘: 

团结是理解社会关系的关键概念。本文探讨了细粒度的团结框架，旨在研究 1867 年至 2022 年间德国议会辩论中对女性和移民的团结态度。我们使用 2,864 个手动标注的文本片段（成本超过 1.8 万欧元）评估了 Llama 3、GPT-3.5 和 GPT-4 等大型语言模型（LLMs）。我们发现 GPT-4 的表现优于其他大型语言模型，其质量接近人类标注水平。利用 GPT-4，我们在 155 年的时间跨度内自动标注了超过 1.8 万个额外实例（成本约为 500 欧元），研究发现：对移民的团结态度多于反对团结的态度，但两者的频率及团结类型随时间发生了变迁。最重要的是，基于群体的（反）团结观念逐渐淡化，取而代之的是关注移民群体脆弱性的同情性团结，以及关注缺乏（经济）贡献的基于交换的反对团结。我们的研究强调了历史事件、社会经济需求与政治意识形态在塑造移民话语和社会凝聚力方面的相互作用。我们还表明，对于高难度的社会科学任务，如果经过精心提示，强大的大型语言模型可以成为替代人工标注的具有成本效益的方案。

[No.167] Fine-Grained Prediction of Reading Comprehension from Eye Movements
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 基于眼动的细粒度阅读理解预测
类别: 
作者: Omer Shubi;Yoav Meiri;Cfir Avraham Hadar;Yevgeni Berzak;
机构: Technion - Israel Institute of Technology;Massachusetts Institute of Technology;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.198/

英摘: Can human reading comprehension be assessed from eye movements in reading? In this work, we address this longstanding question using large-scale eyetracking data over textual materials that are geared towards behavioral analyses of reading comprehension. We focus on a fine-grained and largely unaddressed task of predicting reading comprehension from eye movements at the level of a single question over a passage. We tackle this task using three new multimodal language models, as well as a battery of prior models from the literature. We evaluate the models' ability to generalize to new textual items, new participants, and the combination of both, in two different reading regimes, ordinary reading and information seeking. The evaluations suggest that although the task is highly challenging, eye movements contain useful signals for fine-grained prediction of reading comprehension. Code and data will be made publicly available.
中摘: 

能否通过阅读时的眼动来评估人类的阅读理解能力？在本研究中，我们利用针对阅读理解行为分析而设计的大规模眼动追踪数据，来解决这一长期存在的问题。我们聚焦于一个细粒度且很大程度上尚未被解决的任务，即基于段落，从眼动数据中预测对单个问题的阅读理解能力。我们利用三种新的多模态语言模型，以及文献中的一系列既有模型来处理这一任务。我们评估了模型在两种不同阅读范式（普通阅读和信息寻求）下，向新文本材料、新被试以及两者结合进行泛化的能力。评估结果表明，尽管该任务极具挑战性，但眼动数据包含了用于精细化预测阅读理解的有效信号。代码和数据将对外公开。

[No.168] FineCops-Ref: A new Dataset and Task for Fine-Grained Compositional Referring Expression Comprehension
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: FineCops-Ref：一项新的数据集和任务，用于精细粒度组合指示表达式理解
类别: 
作者: Junzhuo Liu;Xuzheng Yang;Weiwei Li;Peng Wang;
机构: University of Electronic Science and Technology of China;

英摘: Referring Expression Comprehension (REC) is a crucial cross-modal task that objectively evaluates the capabilities of language understanding, image comprehension, and language-to-image grounding. Consequently, it serves as an ideal testing ground for Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs). In pursuit of this goal, we have established a new REC dataset characterized by two key features: Firstly, it is designed with controllable varying levels of difficulty, necessitating multi-level fine-grained reasoning across object categories, attributes, and multi-hop relationships. Secondly, it includes negative text and images created through fine-grained editing and generation based on existing data, thereby testing the model's ability to correctly reject scenarios where the target object is not visible in the image--an essential aspect often overlooked in existing datasets and approaches. Utilizing this high-quality dataset, we conducted comprehensive evaluations of both state-of-the-art specialist models and MLLMs. Our findings indicate that there remains a significant gap in achieving satisfactory grounding performance. We anticipate that our dataset will inspire new approaches to enhance visual reasoning and develop more advanced cross-modal interaction strategies, ultimately unlocking the full potential of MLLMs. Our code and the datasets are available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/FineCops-Ref.
中摘: 

指称表达式理解（REC）是一项至关重要的跨模态任务，能够客观评估语言理解、图像理解以及语言到图像定位的能力。因此，它成为了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的理想测试平台。为实现这一目标，我们构建了一个具有两个关键特征的新 REC 数据集：首先，该数据集设计了可控的、层级不同的难度，要求模型进行跨越对象类别、属性及多跳关系的多层级细粒度推理；其次，它包含了基于现有数据通过细粒度编辑和生成创建的负样本文本和图像，旨在测试模型正确拒绝目标对象在图像中不可见场景的能力——这是现有数据集和方法中常被忽视的一个重要方面。利用该高质量数据集，我们对最先进的专用模型和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）进行了全面评估。我们的研究结果表明，在实现令人满意的语言到图像定位性能方面仍存在显著差距。我们期望我们的数据集能激发新方法以增强视觉推理并开发更先进的跨模态交互策略，从而最终释放多模态大语言模型的全部潜力。我们的代码和数据集可在 https://github.com/liujunzhuo/FineCops-Ref 获取。

[No.169] Finer: Investigating and Enhancing Fine-Grained Visual Concept Recognition in Large Vision Language Models
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 《探究并增强大型视觉语言模型中的细粒度视觉概念识别》
类别: 
作者: Jeonghwan Kim;Heng Ji;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.356/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2402.16315
英摘: Recent advances in instruction-tuned Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have imbued the models with the ability to generate high-level, image-grounded explanations with ease. While such capability is largely attributed to the rich world knowledge contained within the Large Language Models (LLMs), our work reveals their shortcomings in fine-grained visual categorization (FGVC) across six different benchmark settings. Most recent state-of-the-art LVLMs like LLaVa-1.5, InstructBLIP and GPT-4V not only severely deteriorate in terms of classification performance, e.g., average drop of 65.58 in EM for Stanford Dogs for LLaVA-1.5, but also struggle to generate an accurate explanation with detailed attributes based on the concept that appears within an input image despite their capability to generate holistic image-level descriptions. In-depth analyses show that instruction-tuned LVLMs exhibit modality gap, showing discrepancy when given textual and visual inputs that correspond to the same concept, preventing the image modality from leveraging the rich parametric knowledge within the LLMs. In an effort to further the community's endeavor in this direction, we propose a multiple granularity attribute-centric evaluation benchmark, Finer, which aims to establish a ground to evaluate LVLMs' fine-grained visual comprehension ability and provide significantly improved explainability.
中摘: 

指令调优的大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的最新进展赋予了模型轻松生成高层级、基于图像的解释的能力。尽管这种能力主要归功于大语言模型（LLMs）中所包含的丰富世界知识，但我们的工作揭示了它们在六个不同基准设置下的细粒度视觉分类（FGVC）方面的局限性。大多数最新最先进的 LVLMs（如 LLaVa-1.5、InstructBLIP 和 GPT-4V）不仅在分类性能上严重退化，例如 LLaVA-1.5 在斯坦福犬数据集上的 EM 平均下降 65.58，而且尽管具备生成整体图像级描述的能力，却难以根据输入图像中出现的概念生成具有详细属性的准确解释。深入分析表明，指令调优的 LVLMs 存在模态差距，当接收到对应同一概念的文字和视觉输入时会出现差异，导致图像模态无法利用 LLM 内丰富的参数化知识。为推动社区在此方向上的努力，我们提出了一个多粒度属性中心评估基准 Finer，旨在为评估 LVLMs 的细粒度视觉理解能力奠定基础，并提供显著改进的可解释性。

[No.170] FoodieQA: A Multimodal Dataset for Fine-Grained Understanding of Chinese Food Culture

英摘: Food is a rich and varied dimension of cultural heritage, crucial to both individuals and social groups. [No.173] Learning to Refine with Fine-Grained Natural Language Feedback
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 学习通过精细粒度的自然语言反馈进行精炼
类别: 
作者: Manya Wadhwa;Xinyu Zhao;Junyi Jessy Li;Greg Durrett;
机构: University of Texas at Austin;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.716/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.02397
英摘: Recent work has explored the capability of large language models (LLMs) to identify and correct errors in LLM-generated responses. These refinement approaches frequently evaluate what sizes of models are able to do refinement for what problems, but less attention is paid to what effective feedback for refinement looks like. In this work, we propose looking at refinement with feedback as a composition of three distinct LLM competencies: (1) detection of bad generations; (2) fine-grained natural language critique generation; (3) refining with fine-grained feedback. The first step can be implemented with a high-performing discriminative model and steps 2 and 3 can be implemented either via prompted or fine-tuned LLMs. A key property of the proposed Detect, Critique, Refine ("DCR") method is that the step 2 critique model can give fine-grained feedback about errors, made possible by offloading the discrimination to a separate model in step 1. We show that models of different capabilities benefit from refining with DCR on the task of improving factual consistency of document grounded summaries. Overall, DCR consistently outperforms existing end-to-end refinement approaches and current trained models not fine-tuned for factuality critiquing.
中摘: 

近期的研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）识别并纠正 LLM 生成回复中错误的能力。这些修正方法经常评估何种规模的模型能够针对何种问题进行修正，但较少关注有效的修正反馈具体是什么样。在这项工作中，我们提出将带有反馈的修正视为三种不同 LLM 能力的组合：(1) 错误生成的检测；(2) 细粒度自然语言批评的生成；(3) 基于细粒度反馈的修正。第一步可以使用高性能的判别模型实现，而步骤 2 和步骤 3 则可以通过提示或微调的 LLM 来实现。所提出的“检测、批评、修正”（DCR）方法的一个关键特性是，步骤 2 中的批评模型能够提供关于错误的细粒度反馈，这得益于将判别任务卸载到步骤 1 的独立模型。我们证明，不同能力的模型在改进基于文档的摘要的事实一致性任务上，通过 DCR 进行修正均能获益。总体而言，DCR 始终优于现有的端到端修正方法，也优于当前未针对事实性批评进行微调的训练模型。

标题: MedReadMe：医学领域细粒度句子可读性的一项系统性研究

英摘: Medical texts are notoriously challenging to read. Properly measuring their readability is the first step towards making them more accessible. We introduce a new dataset MedReadMe, which consists of manually annotated readability ratings and fine-grained complex span annotation for 4,520 sentences, featuring two novel "Google-Easy" and "Google-Hard" categories. [No.179] MM-MATH: Advancing Multimodal Math Evaluation with Process Evaluation and Fine-grained Classification
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: MM-MATH：通过过程评估与细粒度分类促进多模态数学评估
类别: 
作者: Kai Sun;Yushi Bai;Ji Qi;Lei Hou;Juanzi Li;
机构: Tsinghua University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.73/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2404.05091
英摘: To advance the evaluation of multimodal math reasoning in large multimodal models (LMMs), this paper introduces a novel benchmark, MM-MATH. MM-MATH consists of 5,929 open-ended middle school math problems with visual contexts, with fine-grained classification across difficulty, grade level, and knowledge points. Unlike existing benchmarks relying on binary answer comparison, MM-MATH incorporates both outcome and process evaluations. Process evaluation employs LMM-as-a-judge to automatically analyze solution steps, identifying and categorizing errors into specific error types. Extensive evaluation of ten models on MM-MATH reveals significant challenges for existing LMMs, highlighting their limited utilization of visual information and struggles with higher-difficulty problems. The best-performing model achieves only 31% accuracy on MM-MATH, compared to 82% for humans. This highlights the challenging nature of our benchmark for existing models and the significant gap between the multimodal reasoning capabilities of current models and humans. Our process evaluation reveals that diagram misinterpretation is the most common error, accounting for more than half of the total error cases, underscoring the need for improved image comprehension in multimodal reasoning.
中摘:

[No.180] Multilingual Fine-Grained News Headline Hallucination Detection
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 多语言细粒度新闻标题幻觉检测
类别: 
作者: Jiaming Shen;Tianqi Liu;Jialu Liu;Zhen Qin;Jay Pavagadhi;Simon Baumgartner;Michael Bendersky;
机构: Google;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.461/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2407.15975
英摘: The popularity of automated news headline generation has surged with advancements in pre-trained language models. However, these models often suffer from the ``hallucination'' problem, where the generated headline is not fully supported by its source article. Efforts to address this issue have predominantly focused on English, using over-simplistic classification schemes that overlook nuanced hallucination types. In this study, we introduce the first multilingual, fine-grained news headline hallucination detection dataset that contains over 11 thousand pairs in 5 languages, each annotated with detailed hallucination types by experts. We conduct extensive experiments on this dataset under two settings. First, we implement several supervised fine-tuning approaches as preparatory solutions and demonstrate this dataset's challenges and utilities. Second, we test various large language models' in-context learning abilities and propose two novel techniques, language-dependent demonstration selection and coarse-to-fine prompting, to boost the few-shot hallucination detection performance in terms of the example-F1 metric. We release this dataset to foster further research in multilingual, fine-grained headline hallucination detection.
中摘: 

随着预训练语言模型的发展，自动化新闻标题生成的流行度激增。然而，这些模型常受“幻觉”问题的困扰，即生成的标题无法得到其原文的充分支持。针对这一问题的努力主要集中在英语领域，所使用的分类方案过于简化，忽视了细微的幻觉类型。在本研究中，我们推出了首个多语言、细粒度的新闻标题幻觉检测数据集，该数据集包含 5 种语言的 1.1 万多对样本，每个样本均由专家标注了详细的幻觉类型。我们在两种设置下针对该数据集进行了广泛的实验。首先，我们实施了多种监督微调方法作为初步方案，并展示了该数据集的挑战与实用价值。其次，我们测试了各种大型语言模型的上下文学习能力，并提出两种新颖技术，即语言相关的示例选择和由粗到细的提示，以提升少样本幻觉检测在示例 F1 指标上的性能。我们发布该数据集旨在促进多语言、细粒度标题幻觉检测的进一步研究。

[No.182] Navigating the Nuances: A Fine-grained Evaluation of Vision-Language Navigation
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 细察其中微妙：对视觉语言导航的一种精细评估
类别: 
作者: Zehao Wang;Minye Wu;Yixin Cao;Yubo Ma;Meiqi Chen;Tinne Tuytelaars;

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.269/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17313
英摘: This study presents a novel evaluation framework for the Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) task. It aims to diagnose current models for various instruction categories at a finer-grained level. The framework is structured around the context-free grammar (CFG) of the task. The CFG serves as the basis for the problem decomposition and the core premise of the instruction categories design. We propose a semi-automatic method for CFG construction with the help of Large-Language Models (LLMs). 本研究提出了一种用于视觉语言导航（VLN）任务的新颖评估框架，旨在以更细粒度诊断各类指令类别下的当前模型。该框架围绕任务的上下文无关文法（CFG）构建，CFG 既作为问题分解的基础，也是指令类别设计的核心前提。我们借助大型语言模型（LLMs）提出了一种半自动的 CFG 构建方法。随后，我们归纳并生成了涵盖五个主要指令类别的数据（即方向改变、地标识别、区域识别、垂直移动和数值理解）。对不同模型的分析揭示了显著的性能差异和反复出现的问题。数值理解的停滞、方向概念的严重选择偏差以及其他有趣发现，均有助于未来语言引导导航系统的发展。

[No.183] Pioneering Reliable Assessment in Text-to-Image Knowledge Editing: Leveraging a Fine-Grained Dataset and an Innovative Criterion
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 开创性地在文本到图像知识编辑中实现可靠评估：利用精细粒度数据集和创新标准
类别: 
作者: Hengrui Gu;Kaixiong Zhou;Yili Wang;Ruobing Wang;Xin Wang;
机构: 
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.897/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.17928
英摘: During pre-training, the Text-to-Image (T2I) diffusion models encode factual knowledge into their parameters. These parameterized facts enable realistic image generation, but they may become obsolete over time, thereby misrepresenting the current state of the world. Knowledge editing techniques aim to update model knowledge in a targeted way. However, facing the dual challenges posed by inadequate editing datasets and unreliable evaluation criterion, the development of T2I knowledge editing encounter difficulties in effectively generalizing injected knowledge. In this work, we design a T2I knowledge editing framework by comprehensively spanning on three phases: First, we curate a dataset \textbf{CAKE}, comprising paraphrase and multi-object test, to enable more fine-grained assessment on knowledge generalization. Second, we propose a novel criterion, \textbf{adaptive CLIP threshold}, to effectively filter out false successful images under the current criterion and achieve reliable editing evaluation. Finally, we introduce \textbf{MPE}, a simple but effective approach for T2I knowledge editing. Instead of tuning parameters, MPE precisely recognizes and edits the outdated part of the conditioning text-prompt to accommodate the up-to-date knowledge. A straightforward implementation of MPE (Based on in-context learning) exhibits better overall performance than previous model editors. We hope these efforts can further promote faithful evaluation of T2I knowledge editing methods.
中摘: 

在预训练阶段，文生图（T2I）扩散模型将事实知识编码进其参数中。这些参数化的事实实现了逼真的图像生成，但随着时间推移可能变得过时，从而错误地反映了当前世界的状况。知识编辑技术旨在有针对性地更新模型知识。然而，面对由编辑数据集不足和评估标准不可靠所带来的双重挑战，T2I 知识编辑的发展在有效泛化注入的知识方面面临困难。在本研究中，我们设计了一个全面涵盖三个阶段的 T2I 知识编辑框架：首先，我们构建了一个名为 \textbf{CAKE} 的数据集，包含释义和多对象测试，以实现对知识泛化的更细粒度评估。其次，我们提出了一种新颖的标准——\textbf{自适应 CLIP 阈值}，旨在有效过滤掉在当前标准下误判为成功的图像，从而实现可靠的编辑评估。最后，我们介绍了 \textbf{MPE}，一种简单而有效的 T2I 知识编辑方法。与调整参数不同，MPE 能够精确识别并编辑条件文本提示中过时的部分，以兼容更新后的知识。MPE 的一种基于上下文学习的直接实现，展现了优于以往模型编辑器的整体性能。我们希望这些努力能进一步推动对 T2I 知识编辑方法的忠实评估。

[No.184] SciPrompt: Knowledge-augmented Prompting for Fine-grained Categorization of Scientific Topics
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: SciPrompt：增强知识提示以细粒度分类科学主题
类别: 
作者: Zhiwen You;Kanyao Han;Haotian Zhu;Bertram Ludaescher;Jana Diesner;
机构: University of Illinois Urbana-Champaign;University of Washington;Technical University of Munich;
类型: Main
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.350/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.01946
英摘: Prompt-based fine-tuning has become an essential method for eliciting information encoded in pre-trained language models for a variety of tasks, including text classification. For multi-class classification tasks, prompt-based fine-tuning under low-resource scenarios has resulted in performance levels comparable to those of fully fine-tuning methods. Previous studies have used crafted prompt templates and verbalizers, mapping from the label terms space to the class space, to solve the classification problem as a masked language modeling task. However, cross-domain and fine-grained prompt-based fine-tuning with an automatically enriched verbalizer remains unexplored, mainly due to the difficulty and costs of manually selecting domain label terms for the verbalizer, which requires humans with domain expertise. To address this challenge, we introduce SciPrompt, a framework designed to automatically retrieve scientific topic-related terms for low-resource text classification tasks. To this end, we select semantically correlated and domain-specific label terms within the context of scientific literature for verbalizer augmentation. Furthermore, we propose a new verbalization strategy that uses correlation scores as additional weights to enhance the prediction performance of the language model during model tuning. Our method outperforms state-of-the-art, prompt-based fine-tuning methods on scientific text classification tasks under few and zero-shot settings, especially in classifying fine-grained and emerging scientific topics.
中摘: 

基于提示的微调已成为激发预训练语言模型中编码的信息以应对多种任务（包括文本分类）的关键方法。对于多分类任务，在低资源场景下，基于提示的微调所达到的性能水平可与全参数微调方法相媲美。以往研究采用了精心设计的提示模板和词汇集，通过将标签术语空间映射到类别空间，将分类问题作为掩码语言建模任务来解决。然而，利用自动增强词汇集的跨领域和细粒度基于提示的微调尚未得到探索，主要原因是手动为词汇集选择领域标签术语存在难度和成本，这需要具备领域专业知识的人员。为解决这一挑战，我们提出了 SciPrompt，这是一个专为低资源文本分类任务自动检索科学主题相关术语而设计的框架。为此，我们在科学文献语境下选择语义相关且领域特定的标签术语，以增强词汇集。此外，我们提出了一种新的标签映射策略，利用相关性得分作为附加权重，从而在模型微调期间增强语言模型的预测性能。我们的方法在少样本和零样本设置下的科学文本分类任务上优于最先进的基于提示的微调方法，特别是在对细粒度和新兴科学主题进行分类时。

[No.185] Self-Explore: Enhancing Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Fine-grained Rewards
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 自我探索：通过精细粒度的奖励提升语言模型的数学推理能力
类别: 
作者: Hyeonbin Hwang;Doyoung Kim;Seungone Kim;Seonghyeon Ye;Minjoon Seo;

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.78/

英摘: Training on large amounts of rationales (i.e., CoT Fine-tuning) is effective at improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). However, acquiring human-authored rationales or augmenting rationales from proprietary models is costly and not scalable. In this paper, we study the problem of whether LLMs could self-improve their reasoning capabilities. To this end, we propose Self-Explore, where the LLM is tasked to explore the first wrong step (i.e., the first pit) within the rationale and use such signals as fine-grained rewards for further improvement. 利用大量推理过程（即思维链微调）进行训练，能够有效提升大语言模型（LLMs）的推理能力。然而，获取人工撰写的推理过程或从专有模型增强推理过程成本高昂且不具备可扩展性。在本文中，我们研究了大语言模型是否能够自我改进其推理能力这一问题。为此，我们提出了 Self-Explore，其中大语言模型的任务是探索推理过程中的第一个错误步骤（即第一个陷阱），并将此类信号作为细粒度奖励用于进一步改进。在 GSM8K 和 MATH 测试集上，与监督微调（SFT）相比，Self-Explore 在三个大语言模型上的平均提升分别达到了 11.57% 和 2.89%。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/hbin0701/Self-Explore 获取。

[No.186] TROPE: TRaining-Free Object-Part Enhancement for Seamlessly Improving Fine-Grained Zero-Shot Image Captioning
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: TROPE: 无需训练的对象部分增强方法，无缝提升细粒度零样本图像描述
类别: 
作者: Joshua Feinglass;Yezhou Yang;
机构: Arizona State University;
类型: finding
链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.findings-emnlp.207/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2409.19960
英摘: Zero-shot inference, where pre-trained models perform tasks without specific training data, is an exciting emergent ability of large models like CLIP. Although there has been considerable exploration into enhancing zero-shot abilities in image captioning (IC) for popular datasets such as MSCOCO and Flickr8k, these approaches fall short with fine-grained datasets like CUB, FLO, UCM-Captions, and Sydney-Captions. These datasets require captions to discern between visually and semantically similar classes, focusing on detailed object parts and their attributes. To overcome this challenge, we introduce TRaining-Free Object-Part Enhancement (TROPE). TROPE enriches a base caption with additional object-part details using object detector proposals and Natural Language Processing techniques. It complements rather than alters the base caption, allowing seamless integration with other captioning methods and offering users enhanced flexibility. Our evaluations show that TROPE consistently boosts performance across all tested zero-shot IC approaches and achieves state-of-the-art results on fine-grained IC datasets.
中摘: 

零样本推理是指预训练模型无需特定训练数据即可执行任务，这是 CLIP 等大型模型一种引人注目的涌现能力。尽管已有大量探索致力于增强流行数据集（如 MSCOCO 和 Flickr8k）上的零样本图像描述（IC）能力，但这些方法在细粒度数据集（如 CUB、FLO、UCM-Captions 和 Sydney-Captions）上表现不足。这些数据集要求描述能够区分视觉上相似且语义相近的类别，侧重于详细的物体部件及其属性。为克服这一挑战，我们提出了无需训练的物体部件增强（TRaining-Free Object-Part Enhancement，TROPE）。TROPE 利用物体检测提议和自然语言处理技术为基础描述增添额外的物体部件细节。它对基础描述起到补充而非改变的作用，允许与其他描述方法无缝集成，并为用户提供增强的灵活性。我们的评估表明，TROPE 在所有测试的零样本 IC 方法中均能提升性能，并在细粒度 IC 数据集上取得了最先进（state-of-the-art）的结果。

[No.188] VideoScore: Building Automatic Metrics to Simulate Fine-grained Human Feedback for Video Generation
来源: EMNLP 2024
命中: fine-grained
标题: 视频评分：构建自动指标以模拟视频生成中的细粒度人类反馈
类别: 
作者: Xuan He;Dongfu Jiang;Ge Zhang;Max Ku;Achint Soni;Sherman Siu;Haonan Chen;Abhranil Chandra;Ziyan Jiang;Aaran Arulraj;Kai Wang;Quy Duc Do;Yuansheng Ni;Bohan Lyu;Yaswanth Narsupalli;Rongqi Fan;Zhiheng Lyu;Bill Yuchen Lin;Wenhu Chen;

链接: https://aclanthology.org/2024.emnlp-main.127/
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.15252
英摘: The recent years have witnessed great advances in video generation. However, the development of automatic video metrics is lagging significantly behind. None of the existing metric is able to provide reliable scores over generated videos. The main barrier is the lack of large-scale human-annotated dataset. In this paper, we release VideoFeedback, the first large-scale dataset containing human-provided multi-aspect score over 37.6K synthesized videos from 11 existing video generative models. We train VideoScore (initialized from Mantis) based on VideoFeedback to enable automatic video quality assessment. Experiments show that the Spearman correlation between VideoScore and humans can reach 77.1 on VideoFeedback-test, beating the prior best metrics by about 50 points. Further result on other held-out EvalCrafter, GenAI-Bench, and VBench show that VideoScore has consistently much higher correlation with human judges than other metrics. Due to these results, we believe VideoScore can serve as a great proxy for human raters to (1) rate different video models to track progress (2) simulate fine-grained human feedback in Reinforcement Learning with Human Feedback (RLHF) to improve current video generation models.
中摘: 

近年来，视频生成领域取得了巨大进步。然而，自动视频指标的发展严重滞后。现有的任何指标都无法为生成的视频提供可靠的评分。主要障碍在于缺乏大规模的人类标注数据集。在本文中，我们发布了 VideoFeedback，这是首个包含人类提供的多维度评分的大规模数据集，涵盖来自 11 个现有视频生成模型的 3.76 万个合成视频。我们基于 VideoFeedback 训练了 VideoScore（由 Mantis 初始化），以实现自动视频质量评估。实验表明，在 VideoFeedback-test 上，VideoScore 与人类评分之间的斯皮尔曼相关系数可达 77.1，比之前的最佳指标高出约 50 分。在其他预留的 EvalCrafter、GenAI-Bench 和 VBench 上的进一步结果表明，VideoScore 与其他指标相比，与人类评审的相关性始终高出许多。鉴于这些结果，我们相信 VideoScore 可以作为人类评估者的优秀代理，用于（1）评估不同的视频模型以跟踪进展；（2）在带有人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）中模拟细粒度的人类反馈，从而改进当前的视频生成模型。

[No.189] DNA: Denoised Neighborhood Aggregation for Fine-grained Category Discovery
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: DNA：去噪邻域聚合算法在细粒度类别发现中的应用
类别: 
作者: Wenbin An;Feng Tian;Wenkai Shi;Yan Chen;Qinghua Zheng;QianYing Wang;Ping Chen;
机构: Xi'an Jiao Tong University;Lenovo Group;University of Massachusetts Boston;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=EkftL7NgtW
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.10151
英摘: Discovering fine-grained categories from coarsely labeled data is a practical and challenging task, which can bridge the gap between the demand for fine-grained analysis and the high annotation cost. Previous works mainly focus on instance-level discrimination to learn low-level features, but ignore semantic similarities between data, which may prevent these models learning compact cluster representations. In this paper, we propose Denoised Neighborhood Aggregation (DNA), a self-supervised framework that encodes semantic structures of data into the embedding space. Specifically, we retrieve k-nearest neighbors of a query as its positive keys to capture semantic similarities between data and then aggregate information from the neighbors to learn compact cluster representations, which can make fine-grained categories more separatable. However, the retrieved neighbors can be noisy and contain many false-positive keys, which can degrade the quality of learned embeddings. To cope with this challenge, we propose three principles to filter out these false neighbors for better representation learning. Furthermore, we theoretically justify that the learning objective of our framework is equivalent to a clustering loss, which can capture semantic similarities between data to form compact fine-grained clusters. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets show that our method can retrieve more accurate neighbors (21.31% accuracy improvement) and outperform state-of-the-art models by a large margin (average 9.96% improvement on three metrics). Our code and data are available at https://github.com/Lackel/DNA.
中摘: 

从粗标注数据中发现细粒度类别是一项具有实际意义且充满挑战的任务，这有助于弥合细粒度分析需求与高昂标注成本之间的差距。以往的研究主要关注实例级判别以学习低层特征，但忽略了数据之间的语义相似性，这可能会阻碍这些模型学习到紧凑的聚类表示。在本文中，我们提出了去噪邻居聚合（DNA），这是一种将数据的语义结构编码到嵌入空间中的自监督框架。具体而言，我们检索查询样本的 k 近邻作为其正例键，以捕捉数据间的语义相似性，然后聚合来自邻居的信息以学习紧凑的聚类表示，这可以使细粒度类别更具可分性。然而，检索到的邻居可能含有噪声并包含许多假阳性键，这可能会降低所学嵌入的质量。为了应对这一挑战，我们提出了三个原则来过滤掉这些虚假邻居，以便获得更好的表示学习。此外，我们在理论上证明，本框架的学习目标等价于一个聚类损失，它可以捕捉数据间的语义相似性以形成紧凑的细粒度聚类。在三个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法能够检索到更准确的邻居（准确率提升 21.31%），并以较大优势优于最先进模型（在三个指标上平均提升 9.96%）。我们的代码和数据可在 https://github.com/Lackel/DNA 获取。

[No.190] A Fine-Grained Taxonomy of Replies to Hate Speech

[No.191] Discourse Structures Guided Fine-grained Propaganda Identification
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 引导下的细粒度 propaganda 识别话语结构
类别: 
作者: Yuanyuan Lei;Ruihong Huang;
机构: Texas A&M University;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=xbnNgqGefc
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.18544
英摘: Propaganda is a form of deceptive narratives that instigate or mislead the public, usually with a political purpose. In this paper, we aim to identify propaganda in political news at two fine-grained levels: sentence-level and token-level. We observe that propaganda content is more likely to be embedded in sentences that attribute causality or assert contrast to nearby sentences, as well as seen in opinionated evaluation, speculation and discussions of future expectation. Hence, we propose to incorporate both local and global discourse structures for propaganda discovery and construct two teacher models for identifying PDTB-style discourse relations between nearby sentences and common discourse roles of sentences in a news article respectively. We further devise two methods to incorporate the two types of discourse structures for propaganda identification by either using teacher predicted probabilities as additional features or soliciting guidance in a knowledge distillation framework. Experiments on the benchmark dataset demonstrate that leveraging guidance from discourse structures can significantly improve both precision and recall of propaganda content identification.
中摘: 

宣传是一种旨在煽动或误导公众的欺骗性叙事形式，通常具有政治目的。本文旨在从两个细粒度层面识别政治新闻中的宣传：句子层面和词元层面。我们观察到，宣传内容更可能嵌入于那些与邻近句子建立因果联系或断言对比关系的句子中，同时也常见于观点性评估、推测以及对未来预期的讨论中。因此，我们提出整合局部和全局话语结构以进行宣传发现，并构建两个教师模型，分别用于识别邻近句子之间的 PDTB 风格话语关系以及新闻文章中句子的常见话语角色。我们进一步设计了两种方法来整合这两种类型的话语结构以进行宣传识别，即要么将教师预测概率作为附加特征，要么在知识蒸馏框架中寻求指导。基准数据集上的实验表明，利用话语结构的指导可以显著提高宣传内容识别的精确率和召回率。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.192] ReSee: Responding through Seeing Fine-grained Visual Knowledge in Open-domain Dialogue
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: ReSee：通过观察开放领域对话中的细粒度视觉知识进行回应
类别: 
作者: Haoqin Tu;Yitong Li;Fei Mi;Zhongliang Yang;
机构: University of Chinese Academy of Sciences;Huawei;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=Xd2A31vcLd
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.13602
英摘: Incorporating visual knowledge into text-only dialogue systems has become a potential direction to imitate the way humans think, imagine, and communicate. However, existing multimodal dialogue systems are either confined by the scale and quality of available datasets or the coarse concept of visual knowledge. To address these issues, we provide a new paradigm of constructing multimodal dialogues as well as two datasets extended from text-only dialogues under such paradigm (ReSee-WoW, ReSee-DD). We propose to explicitly split the visual knowledge into finer granularity (``turn-level'' and ``entity-level''). To further boost the accuracy and diversity of augmented visual information, we retrieve them from the Internet or a large image dataset. To demonstrate the superiority and universality of the provided visual knowledge, we propose a simple but effective framework ReSee to add visual representation into vanilla dialogue models by modality concatenations. We also conduct extensive experiments and ablations w.r.t. different model configurations and visual knowledge settings. Empirical, encouraging results not only demonstrate the effectiveness of introducing visual knowledge at both entity and turn level but also verify the proposed model ReSee outperforms several state-of-the-art methods on automatic and human evaluations. By leveraging text and vision knowledge, ReSee can produce informative responses with real-world visual concepts. Our code is available at https://github.com/ImKeTT/ReSee.
中摘: 

将视觉知识融入到纯文本对话系统中，已成为模仿人类思考、想象及交流方式的一个潜在方向。然而，现有的多模态对话系统要么受限于可用数据集的规模和质量，要么受限于视觉知识的粗粒度概念。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种构建多模态对话的新范式，并基于该范式提供了两个从纯文本对话扩展而来的数据集（ReSee-WoW, ReSee-DD）。我们提出将视觉知识明确地细分为更细的粒度（即“回合级”和“实体级”）。为了进一步提升增强视觉信息的准确性和多样性，我们从互联网或大型图像数据集中检索这些信息。为了展示所提供视觉知识的优越性和普适性，我们提出了一个简单而有效的框架 ReSee，通过模态拼接将视觉表示融入基础对话模型中。我们还针对不同模型配置和视觉知识设置进行了广泛的实验及消融研究。实证且令人鼓舞的结果不仅证明了在实体级和回合级引入视觉知识的有效性，还验证了所提出的模型 ReSee 在自动和人工评估上优于几种最先进的方法。通过利用文本和视觉知识，ReSee 能够生成包含真实世界视觉概念的信息丰富的回复。我们的代码可在 https://github.com/ImKeTT/ReSee 获取。

[No.193] Enhancing Low-resource Fine-grained Named Entity Recognition by Leveraging Coarse-grained Datasets
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过利用粗粒度数据集增强低资源细粒度命名实体识别
类别: 
作者: Su Ah Lee;Seokjin Oh;Woohwan Jung;
机构: Hanyang University;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=nIp7wkMeMP
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.11715
英摘: Named Entity Recognition (NER) frequently suffers from the problem of insufficient labeled data, particularly in fine-grained NER scenarios. Although $K$-shot learning techniques can be applied, their performance tends to saturate when the number of annotations exceeds several tens of labels. To overcome this problem, we utilize existing coarse-grained datasets that offer a large number of annotations. A straightforward approach to address this problem is pre-finetuning, which employs coarse-grained data for representation learning. However, it cannot directly utilize the relationships between fine-grained and coarse-grained entities, although a fine-grained entity type is likely to be a subcategory of a coarse-grained entity type. We propose a fine-grained NER model with a Fine-to-Coarse(F2C) mapping matrix to leverage the hierarchical structure explicitly. In addition, we present an inconsistency filtering method to eliminate coarse-grained entities that are inconsistent with fine-grained entity types to avoid performance degradation. Our experimental results show that our method outperforms both $K$-shot learning and supervised learning methods when dealing with a small number of fine-grained annotations.
中摘: 

命名实体识别（NER）经常面临标注数据不足的问题，特别是在细粒度 NER 场景下。尽管可以应用 K-shot 学习技术，但当标注数量超过几十个时，其性能往往趋于饱和。为了解决这一问题，我们利用现有的粗粒度数据集，这些数据集提供了大量标注。解决这一问题的直接方法是预微调，该方法利用粗粒度数据进行表示学习。然而，尽管细粒度实体类型很可能是粗粒度实体类型的子类，该方法却无法直接利用细粒度与粗粒度实体之间的关系。我们提出了一种带有细到粗（F2C）映射矩阵的细粒度 NER 模型，以显式地利用层次结构。此外，我们提出了一种不一致性过滤方法，用于剔除那些与细粒度实体类型不一致的粗粒度实体，从而避免性能下降。实验结果表明，在处理少量细粒度标注时，我们的方法优于 K-shot 学习和监督学习方法。

[No.195] FActScore: Fine-grained Atomic Evaluation of Factual Precision in Long Form Text Generation
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 细粒度原子事实评分：长文本生成中的精细事实精确度评估
类别: 
作者: Sewon Min;Kalpesh Krishna;Xinxi Lyu;Mike Lewis;Wen-tau Yih;Pang Wei Koh;Mohit Iyyer;Luke Zettlemoyer;Hannaneh Hajishirzi;
机构: Meta;University of Massachusetts Amherst;University of Washington;Google;
类型: Long Main
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=GLA4ablO3M
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.14251
英摘: Evaluating the factuality of long-form text generated by large language models (LMs) is non-trivial because (1) generations often contain a mixture of supported and unsupported pieces of information, making binary judgments of quality inadequate, and (2) human evaluation is time-consuming and costly. In this paper, we introduce FACTSCORE, a new evaluation that breaks a generation into a series of atomic facts and computes the percentage of atomic facts supported by a reliable knowledge source. We conduct an extensive human evaluation to obtain FACTSCOREs of people biographies generated by several state-of-the-art commercial LMs -- InstructGPT, ChatGPT, and the retrieval-augmented PerplexityAI -- and report new analysis demonstrating the need for such a fine-grained score (e.g., ChatGPT only achieves 58%). Since human evaluation is costly, we also introduce an automated model that estimates FACTSCORE using retrieval and a strong language model, with less than a 2% error rate. Finally, we use this automated metric to evaluate 6,500 generations from a new set of 13 recent LMs that would have cost $26K if evaluated by humans, with various findings: GPT-4 and ChatGPT are more factual than public models, and Vicuna and Alpaca are some of the best public models. FACTSCORE is available for public use via `pip install factscore`.
中摘: 

评估大型语言模型（LMs）生成长文的事实性并非易事，原因有二：(1) 生成内容通常包含混合的有据可依和无据可依的信息，使得对质量的二元判定不足；(2) 人类评估耗时且昂贵。在本文中，我们介绍了 FACTSCORE，这是一种新的评估方法，它将生成内容分解为一系列原子事实，并计算由可靠知识源支持的原子事实的百分比。我们进行了广泛的人类评估，以获取由几种最先进的商用大型语言模型——InstructGPT、ChatGPT 以及检索增强型的 PerplexityAI——生成的人物传记的 FACTSCORE，并报告了新的分析，证明了这种细粒度评分的必要性（例如，ChatGPT 仅达到 58%）。由于人类评估成本高昂，我们还引入了一种自动化模型，该模型利用检索技术和强大的语言模型来估算 FACTSCORE，错误率低于 2%。最后，我们使用这一自动化指标评估了来自 13 个最新大型语言模型的 6,500 条生成内容，若由人类评估将花费 2.6 万美元，并得出了多项发现：GPT-4 和 ChatGPT 比公开模型更具事实性，而 Vicuna 和 Alpaca 是表现最佳的公开模型之一。FACTSCORE 可通过 `pip install factscore` 公开使用。

[No.196] DeltaScore: Fine-Grained Story Evaluation with Perturbations
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: DeltaScore：基于扰动的细粒度故事评估
类别: 
作者: Zhuohan Xie;Miao Li;Trevor Cohn;Jey Han Lau;
机构: University of Melbourne;
类型: Long Findings
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=hDzfqmLrol
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2303.08991
英摘: Numerous evaluation metrics have been developed for natural language generation tasks, but their effectiveness in evaluating stories is limited as they are not specifically tailored to assess intricate aspects of storytelling, such as fluency and interestingness. In this paper, we introduce DELTASCORE, a novel methodology that employs perturbation techniques for the evaluation of nuanced story aspects. Our central proposition posits that the extent to which a story excels in a specific aspect (e.g., fluency) correlates with the magnitude of its susceptibility to particular perturbations (e.g., the introduction of typos). Given this, we measure the quality of an aspect by calculating the likelihood difference between pre- and post-perturbation states using pre-trained language models. We compare DELTASCORE with existing metrics on storytelling datasets from two domains in five fine-grained story aspects: fluency, coherence, relatedness, logicality, and interestingness. DELTASCORE demonstrates remarkable performance, revealing a surprising finding that a specific perturbation proves highly effective in capturing multiple aspects.
中摘: 

尽管为自然语言生成任务开发了许多评估指标，但其在评估故事时的有效性有限，因为这些指标并未专门针对评估讲故事的复杂方面（如流畅性和趣味性）而设计。在本文中，我们引入了 DELTASCORE，这是一种新颖的方法，采用扰动技术来评估故事细微的方面。我们的核心主张是，故事在特定方面（例如流畅性）表现优异的程度，与其对特定扰动（例如引入错别字）的敏感程度密切相关。基于此，我们利用预训练语言模型，通过计算扰动前后状态的似然概率差异来衡量某一方面的质量。我们在来自两个领域的故事数据集上，从五个细粒度的故事方面（流畅性、连贯性、关联性、逻辑性和趣味性）将 DELTASCORE 与现有指标进行了比较。DELTASCORE 展现了卓越的性能，揭示了一个令人惊讶的发现：特定的扰动被证明在捕捉多个方面上极为有效。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------

[No.198] Learning to Correct Noisy Labels for Fine-Grained Entity Typing via Co-Prediction Prompt Tuning
来源: EMNLP 2023
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过协同预测提示调优学习纠正细粒度实体类型的嘈杂标签
类别: 
作者: Minghao Tang;Yongquan He;Yongxiu Xu;Hongbo Xu;Wenyuan Zhang;Yang Lin;
机构: Chinese Academy of Sciences;Meituan;Institute of Information Engineering;
类型: Long Findings
链接: https://openreview.net/forum?id=euYA3EmI0e
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2310.14596
英摘: Fine-grained entity typing (FET) is an essential task in natural language processing that aims to assign semantic types to entities in text. However, FET poses a major challenge known as the noise labeling problem, whereby current methods rely on estimating noise distribution to identify noisy labels but are confused by diverse noise distribution deviation. To address this limitation, we introduce Co-Prediction Prompt Tuning for noise correction in FET, which leverages multiple prediction results to identify and correct noisy labels. Specifically, we integrate prediction results to recall labeled labels and utilize a differentiated margin to identify inaccurate labels. Moreover, we design an optimization objective concerning divergent co-predictions during fine-tuning, ensuring that the model captures sufficient information and maintains robustness in noise identification. Experimental results on three widely-used FET datasets demonstrate that our noise correction approach significantly enhances the quality of various types of training samples, including those annotated using distant supervision, ChatGPT, and crowdsourcing.
中摘: 

细粒度实体类型化（FET）是自然语言处理领域的一项关键任务，旨在为文本中的实体分配语义类型。然而，FET 面临一个被称为噪声标注问题的重大挑战，当前方法依赖于估计噪声分布来识别噪声标签，却受困于多样化的噪声分布偏差。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种用于 FET 噪声校正的协同预测提示微调方法，该方法利用多个预测结果来识别并校正噪声标签。具体而言，我们整合预测结果以恢复已标注标签，并利用差异化边界来识别不准确标签。此外，我们设计了一个针对微调期间发散协同预测的优化目标，确保模型能够捕捉充分的信息，并在噪声识别中保持鲁棒性。在三个广泛使用的 FET 数据集上的实验结果表明，我们的噪声校正方法显著提升了各类训练样本的质量，包括那些采用远监督、ChatGPT 和众包进行标注的样本。

[No.201] Real-Time Calibration Model for Low-Cost Sensor in Fine-Grained Time Series
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 低成本传感器在细粒度时间序列中的实时校准模型
类别: application domains
作者: Seokho Ahn;Hyungjin Kim;Sungbok Shin;Young-Duk Seo;
机构: Inha University;INRIA;University of Maryland;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/31974
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.20170
英摘: Precise measurements from sensors are crucial, but data is usually collected from low-cost, low-tech systems, which are often inaccurate. Thus, they require further calibrations. To that end, we first identify three requirements for effective calibration under practical low-tech sensor conditions. Based on the requirements, we develop a model called TESLA, Transformer for effective sensor calibration utilizing logarithmic-binned attention. TESLA uses a high-performance deep learning model, Transformers, to calibrate and capture non-linear components. At its core, it employs logarithmic binning to minimize attention complexity. TESLA achieves consistent real-time calibration, even with longer sequences and finer-grained time series in hardware-constrained systems. Experiments show that TESLA outperforms existing novel deep learning and newly crafted linear models in accuracy, calibration speed, and energy efficiency.
中摘: 

传感器的精确测量至关重要，但数据通常采集自低成本、低技术系统，这些系统往往不够精确。因此，需要对其进行进一步校准。为此，我们首先明确了在实际低技术传感器环境下实现有效校准的三个要求。基于这些要求，我们开发了一款名为 TESLA 的模型，即利用对数分箱注意力进行有效传感器校准的 Transformer。TESLA 采用高性能深度学习模型 Transformer 来实现校准并捕捉非线性成分。其核心机制采用对数分箱技术，以最小化注意力复杂度。即便在硬件受限系统中，面对更长的序列和更细粒度的时间序列，TESLA 仍能实现一致的实时校准。实验表明，TESLA 在准确性、校准速度以及能效方面均优于现有的新型深度学习模型和新构建的线性模型。

[No.204] Learning Fine-grained Domain Generalization via Hyperbolic State Space Hallucination
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过双曲状态空间幻觉实现细粒度领域泛化学习
类别: computer vision i
作者: Qi Bi;Jingjun Yi;Haolan Zhan;Wei Ji;Gui-Song Xia;
机构: Wuhan University;Monash University;Yale University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32180
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08020
英摘: Fine-grained domain generalization (FGDG) aims to learn a fine-grained representation that can be well generalized to unseen target domains when only trained on the source domain data. Compared with generic domain generalization, FGDG is particularly challenging in that the fine-grained category can be only discerned by some subtle and tiny patterns. Such patterns are particularly fragile under the cross-domain style shifts caused by illumination, color and etc. To push this frontier, this paper presents a novel Hyperbolic State Space Hallucination (HSSH) method. It consists of two key components, namely, state space hallucination (SSH) and hyperbolic manifold consistency (HMC). SSH enriches the style diversity for the state embeddings by firstly extrapolating and then hallucinating the source images. Then, the pre- and post- style hallucinate state embeddings are projected into the hyperbolic manifold. The hyperbolic state space models the high-order statistics, and allows a better discernment of the fine-grained patterns. Finally, the hyperbolic distance is minimized, so that the impact of style variation on fine-grained patterns can be eliminated. Experiments on three FGDG benchmarks demonstrate its state-of-the-art performance.
中摘: 

细粒度领域泛化（FGDG）旨在学习一种细粒度表示，使其在仅基于源域数据进行训练的情况下，仍能很好地泛化到未见过的目标领域。与通用领域泛化相比，FGDG 具有更大的挑战性，因为细粒度类别仅能通过一些细微且微小的模式进行辨别。此类模式在由光照、颜色等引起的跨域风格偏移下尤为脆弱。为了推进这一前沿，本文提出了一种新颖的双曲状态空间幻觉（HSSH）方法。该方法包含两个关键组件，即状态空间幻觉（SSH）和双曲流形一致性（HMC）。SSH 通过首先对源图像进行外推，随后进行幻觉生成，从而丰富了状态嵌入的风格多样性。随后，风格幻觉前后的状态嵌入被投影到双曲流形中。双曲状态空间用于建模高阶统计信息，并允许更好地辨别细粒度模式。最后，通过最小化双曲距离，消除了风格变化对细粒度模式的影响。在三个 FGDG 基准上的实验证明了其拥有最先进的性能。

[No.208] DAMPER: A Dual-Stage Medical Report Generation Framework with Coarse-Grained MeSH Alignment and Fine-Grained Hypergraph Matching
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 阻尼器：一种具有粗粒度MeSH对齐和细粒度超图匹配的双阶段医学报告生成框架
类别: computer vision iii
作者: Xiaofei Huang;Wenting Chen;Jie Liu;Qisheng Lu;Xiaoling Luo;Linlin Shen;
机构: Shenzhen University;City University of Hong Kong;Guangdong Provincial Key Laboratory of Intelligent Information Processing;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32393
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.14535
英摘: Medical report generation is crucial for clinical diagnosis and patient management, summarizing diagnoses and recommendations based on medical imaging. However, existing work often overlook the clinical pipeline involved in report writing, where physicians typically conduct an initial quick review followed by a detailed examination. Moreover, current alignment methods may lead to misaligned relationships. To address these issues, we propose DAMPER, a dual-stage framework for medical report generation that mimics the clinical pipeline of report writing in two stages. In the first stage, a MeSH-Guided Coarse-Grained Alignment (MCG) stage that aligns chest X-ray (CXR) image features with medical subject headings (MeSH) features to generate a rough keyphrase representation of the overall impression. In the second stage, a Hypergraph-Enhanced Fine-Grained Alignment (HFG) stage that constructs hypergraphs for image patches and report annotations, modeling high-order relationships within each modality and performing hypergraph matching to capture semantic correlations between image regions and textual phrases. Finally,the coarse-grained visual features, generated MeSH representations, and visual hypergraph features are fed into a report decoder to produce the final medical report. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of DAMPER in generating comprehensive and accurate medical reports, outperforming state-of-the-art methods across various evaluation metrics.
中摘: 

医疗报告生成对于临床诊断和患者管理至关重要，其基于医学影像总结诊断结果与建议。然而，现有工作往往忽视了报告撰写过程中涉及的临床流程，即医生通常先进行初步的快速审查，随后再进行详细检查。此外，现有的对齐方法可能导致关系错位。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了 DAMPER，这是一个用于医疗报告生成的双阶段框架，分两个阶段模拟报告撰写的临床流程。第一阶段为 MeSH 引导的粗粒度对齐（MCG）阶段，该阶段将胸部 X 光（CXR）图像特征与医学主题词（MeSH）特征对齐，以生成总体印象的粗略关键词表示。第二阶段为超图增强的细粒度对齐（HFG）阶段，该阶段为图像块和报告注释构建超图，对每个模态内的高阶关系进行建模，并执行超图匹配，以捕捉图像区域与文本短语之间的语义相关性。最后，将粗粒度视觉特征、生成的 MeSH 表示以及视觉超图特征输入到报告解码器中，以生成最终的医疗报告。在公共数据集上进行的广泛实验证明了 DAMPER 在生成全面且准确的医疗报告方面的有效性，其在各项评估指标上均优于最先进的方法。

[No.209] SeFAR: Semi-supervised Fine-grained Action Recognition with Temporal Perturbation and Learning Stabilization
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 半监督细粒度动作识别：通过时间扰动和学习稳定化
类别: computer vision iii
作者: Yongle Huang;Haodong Chen;Zhenbang Xu;Zihan Jia;Haozhou Sun;Dian Shao;
机构: Northwestern Polytechnical University;
类型: Technical

arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.01245
英摘: Human action understanding is crucial for the advancement of multimodal systems. While recent developments, driven by powerful large language models (LLMs), aim to be general enough to cover a wide range of categories, they often overlook the need for more specific capabilities. In this work, we address the more challenging task of Fine-grained Action Recognition (FAR), which focuses on detailed semantic labels within shorter temporal duration (e.g., "salto backward tucked with 1 turn"). It also outperforms other semi-supervised methods on two classical coarse-grained datasets, UCF101 and HMDB51. Further analysis and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our designs. Additionally, we show that the features extracted by our SeFAR could largely promote the ability of multimodal foundation models to understand fine-grained and domain-specific semantics.
中摘: 

人类动作理解对于多模态系统的发展至关重要。虽然近年来由强大大型语言模型（LLMs）驱动的最新进展旨在具备足够的通用性以涵盖广泛类别，但它们往往忽视了对更具体能力的需求。在本文中，我们研究了更具挑战性的细粒度动作识别（FAR）任务，该任务关注较短时间跨度内的详细语义标签（例如，“向后翻腾抱膝转体一周”）。鉴于标注细粒度标签的高成本以及微调大型语言模型所需的巨大数据量，我们提出采用半监督学习（SSL）。我们的框架 SeFAR 集成了多项创新设计以应对这些挑战。具体而言，为了捕捉充足的视觉细节，我们构建了双层级时间元素作为更有效的表示，并基于此针对教师 - 学生学习范式设计了一种新的强增强策略，该策略引入了适度的时间扰动。此外，为了应对教师模型在 FAR 预测中的高不确定性，我们提出了自适应调节机制以稳定学习过程。实验表明，SeFAR 在两个 FAR 数据集 FineGym 和 FineDiving 上，跨越不同的数据范围，均达到了最先进水平。它还在两个经典粗粒度数据集 UCF101 和 HMDB51 上优于其他半监督方法。进一步的分析和消融实验验证了我们设计的有效性。此外，我们展示了 SeFAR 提取的特征能够显著提升多模态基础模型理解细粒度及领域特定语义的能力。

中摘: 

视频到音频（V2A）生成利用纯视觉视频特征生成与场景相符的逼真声音。然而，现有的 V2A 模型往往缺乏对生成音频的细粒度控制，特别是在响度变化和整合多模态条件方面。为了克服这些局限，我们提出了 Tri-Ergon，这是一种基于扩散模型的 V2A 架构，它融合了文本、听觉以及像素级视觉提示，以实现细节丰富且语义丰富的音频合成。此外，我们引入了相对于满刻度的响度单位（LUFS）嵌入，该技术允许对单个音频通道的响度随时间变化进行精确的手动控制，从而使我们的模型能够有效应对真实世界拟音（Foley）工作流程中视频与音频之间复杂的关联。Tri-Ergon 能够生成长度各异（最长可达 60 秒）的 44.1 kHz 高保真立体声音频片段，显著优于现有的最先进 V2A 方法，后者通常生成长度固定的单声道音频。

[No.212] Multi-scale Activation, Refinement, and Aggregation: Exploring Diverse Cues for Fine-Grained Bird Recognition
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 多尺度激活、细化和聚合：探索多样线索进行细粒度鸟类识别
类别: computer vision ix
作者: Zhicheng Zhang;Hao Tang;Jinhui Tang;
机构: Nanjing University of Science and Technology;Hong Kong Polytechnic University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/33127
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.09215
英摘: Given the critical role of birds in ecosystems, Fine-Grained Bird Recognition (FGBR) has gained increasing attention, particularly in distinguishing birds within similar subcategories. Although Vision Transformer (ViT)-based methods often outperform Convolutional Neural Network (CNN)-based methods in FGBR, recent studies reveal that the limited receptive field of plain ViT model hinders representational richness and makes them vulnerable to scale variance. Thus, enhancing the multi-scale capabilities of existing ViT-based models to overcome this bottleneck in FGBR is a worthwhile pursuit. In this paper, we propose a novel framework for FGBR, namely Multi-scale Diverse Cues Modeling (MDCM), which explores diverse cues at different scales across various stages of a multi-scale Vision Transformer (MS-ViT) in an "Activation-Selection-Aggregation" paradigm. Specifically, we first propose a multi-scale cue activation module to ensure the discriminative cues learned at different stage are mutually different. Subsequently, a multi-scale token selection mechanism is proposed to remove redundant noise and highlight discriminative, scale-specific cues at each stage. Finally, the selected tokens from each stage are independently utilized for bird recognition, and the recognition results from multiple stages are adaptively fused through a multi-scale dynamic aggregation mechanism for final model decisions. Both qualitative and quantitative results demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed MDCM, which outperforms CNN- and ViT-based models on several widely-used FGBR benchmarks.
中摘: 

鉴于鸟类在生态系统中发挥着关键作用，细粒度鸟类识别（FGBR）已日益受到关注，尤其是在区分相似子类别中的鸟类方面。尽管基于视觉 Transformer (ViT) 的方法通常在细粒度鸟类识别（FGBR）任务中优于基于卷积神经网络 (CNN) 的方法，但近期研究表明，普通 ViT 模型有限的感受野限制了其表示的丰富性，使其对尺度变化敏感。因此，增强现有基于 ViT 模型的多尺度能力，以克服 FGBR 中的这一瓶颈，是一个值得追求的目标。在本文中，我们提出了一种用于 FGBR 的新框架，即多尺度多样线索建模（Multi-scale Diverse Cues Modeling, MDCM），该框架在“激活 - 选择 - 聚合”范式下，探索多尺度视觉 Transformer（MS-ViT）各个阶段中不同尺度的多样线索。具体而言，我们首先提出了一种多尺度线索激活模块，以确保在不同阶段学习到的判别性线索彼此互异。随后，提出了一种多尺度 token 选择机制，旨在去除冗余噪声，并突出每个阶段的判别性、特定于尺度的线索。最后，各阶段选择的 token 被独立用于鸟类识别，而来自多个阶段的识别结果则通过多尺度动态聚合机制自适应地融合，以形成最终的模型决策。定性和定量结果均证明了所提出的 MDCM 框架的有效性，该框架在几个广泛使用的 FGBR 基准测试上优于基于 CNN 和 ViT 的模型。

[No.217] ResMaster: Mastering High-Resolution Image Generation via Structural and Fine-Grained Guidance
来源: AAAI 2025

英摘: Diffusion models excel at producing high-quality images; however, scaling to higher resolutions, such as 4K, often results in over-smoothed content, structural distortions, and repetitive patterns. To this end, we introduce ResMaster, a novel, training-free method that empowers resolution-limited diffusion models to generate high-quality images beyond resolution restrictions. Specifically, ResMaster leverages a low-resolution reference image created by a pre-trained diffusion model to provide structural and fine-grained guidance for crafting high-resolution images on a patch-by-patch basis. To ensure a coherent global structure, ResMaster meticulously aligns the low-frequency components of high-resolution patches with the low-resolution reference at each denoising step. This approach could significantly mitigate local pattern distortions and improve detail refinement. Extensive experiments validate that ResMaster sets a new benchmark for high-resolution image generation and demonstrates promising efficiency. 扩散模型擅长生成高质量图像；然而，扩展至更高分辨率（例如 4K）时，往往会导致内容过度平滑、结构失真以及重复图案。为此，我们提出了 ResMaster，这是一种新颖的无训练方法，旨在使分辨率受限的扩散模型能够生成突破分辨率限制的高质量图像。具体而言，ResMaster 利用由预训练扩散模型生成的低分辨率参考图像，为逐块生成高分辨率图像提供结构性和细粒度引导。为了确保全局结构的连贯性，ResMaster 在每个去噪步骤中，将高分辨率分块的低频分量与低分辨率参考进行精确对齐。为实现细粒度引导，该方法结合了基于低分辨率参考定制的图像提示，以及由视觉 - 语言模型生成的增强型文本提示。该方法能显著减轻局部图案失真，并提升细节的精细化程度。大量实验验证了 ResMaster 为高分辨率图像生成树立了新标杆，并展现了令人期待的高效性。项目页面位于 https://shuweis.github.io/ResMaster。

[No.218] TextToucher: Fine-Grained Text-to-Touch Generation
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: TextToucher: 细粒度文本到触感生成
类别: computer vision vi

Furthermore, we propose a Contrastive Text-Touch Pre-training (CTTP) metric to precisely evaluate the quality of text-driven generated tactile data. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our TextToucher method. The source codes will be available at \url{https://github.com/TtuHamg/TextToucher}.
中摘: 

触觉感知在多模态大模型和具身智能的发展中起着至关重要的作用。为了以尽可能低的成本收集触觉数据，一系列研究尝试通过视觉到触觉的图像翻译来生成触觉图像。然而，与文本模态相比，视觉模态驱动的触觉生成无法准确表征人类的触觉感知。在这项工作中，我们从两个粒度详细分析了触觉图像的特征：物体级（触觉纹理、触觉形状）和传感器级（凝胶状态）。我们通过文本描述对这些信息粒度进行建模，并提出一种细粒度文本到触觉生成方法（TextToucher）来生成高质量的触觉样本。具体而言，我们引入多模态大语言模型来构建关于物体级触觉信息的文本描述，并采用一组可学习文本提示来表示传感器级触觉信息。为了更好地利用构建的文本信息指导触觉生成过程，我们融合了双粒度文本信息，并在扩散 Transformer 架构内探索了多种双粒度文本条件化方法。此外，我们提出了一种对比文本 - 触觉预训练（CTTP）评估指标，用于精确评估文本驱动生成的触觉数据的质量。大量实验证明了我们 TextToucher 方法的优越性。源代码将在 https://github.com/TtuHamg/TextToucher 获取。
----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
[No.219] Enhancing Fine-Grained Vision-Language Pretraining with Negative Augmented Samples
来源: AAAI 2025
命中: fine-grained
标题: 通过负增强样本提升细粒度的视觉-语言预训练
类别: computer vision vii
作者: Yeyuan Wang;Dehong Gao;Lei Yi;Linbo Jin;Jinxia Zhang;Libin Yang;Xiaoyan Cai;
机构: Northwestern Polytechnical University;Alibaba Group;Southeast University;
类型: Technical
链接: https://ojs.aaai.org/index.php/AAAI/article/view/32869
arXi: https://arxiv.org/abs/2412.10029
英摘: Existing Vision-Language Pretraining (VLP) methods have achieved remarkable improvements across a variety of vision-language tasks, confirming their effectiveness in capturing coarse-grained semantic correlations. However, their capability for fine-grained understanding, which is critical for many nuanced vision-language applications, remains limited. Prevailing VLP models often overlook the intricate distinctions in expressing different modal features and typically depend on the similarity of holistic features for cross-modal interactions. Moreover, these models directly align and integrate features from different modalities, focusing more on coarse-grained general representations, thus failing to capture the nuanced differences necessary for tasks demanding a more detailed perception. In response to these limitations, we introduce Negative Augmented Samples(NAS), a refined vision-language pretraining model that innovatively incorporates NAS to specifically address the challenge of fine-grained understanding. NAS utilizes a Visual Dictionary(VD) as a semantic bridge between visual and linguistic domains. Additionally, it employs a Negative Visual Augmentation(NVA) method based on the VD to generate challenging negative image samples. These samples deviate from positive samples exclusively at the token level, thereby necessitating that the model discerns the subtle disparities between positive and negative samples with greater precision. Comprehensive experiments validate the efficacy of NAS components and underscore its potential to enhance fine-grained vision-language comprehension.
中摘: 

现有的视觉 - 语言预训练（VLP）方法在各种视觉 - 语言任务中均取得了显著进步，证实了其在捕捉粗粒度语义关联方面的有效性。然而，其在细粒度理解方面的能力仍然有限，而这对于许多需要细微区分的视觉 - 语言应用至关重要。主流的 VLP 模型通常忽视了表达不同模态特征时的细微区别，往往依赖整体特征的相似性进行跨模态交互。此外，这些模型直接对齐并整合来自不同模态的特征，更侧重于粗粒度的通用表征，因而未能捕捉到对需要更细致感知的任务而言至关重要的细微差异。为应对这些局限性，我们提出了负样本增强（NAS），这是一种精细化的视觉 - 语言预训练模型，它创新性地融合了 NAS 技术以专门解决细粒度理解的挑战。NAS 利用视觉字典（VD）作为视觉和语言领域之间的语义桥梁。同时，它采用一种基于 VD 的负视觉增强（NVA）方法来生成具有挑战性的负样本图像。这些样本仅在词元级别与正样本不同，从而要求模型以更高的精度来辨别正负样本之间的细微差异。全面的实验验证了 NAS 组件的有效性，并强调了其在提升细粒度视觉 - 语言理解方面的潜力。
